<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patrick Stewart has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 12:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 661</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50294702'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died. His work helped redefine leadership in science fiction and mentored generations of actors. The news prompts reflection on a prolific career that bridged stage, screen, and iconic franchises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。其横跨舞台、电视和电影的职业生涯对科幻与表演行业产生深远影响。人们由此缅怀这位多产演员及其对文化的贡献。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50319510' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50319510' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://karpathy.bearblog.dev/hacker-tube/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HackerTube: YouTube built from videos recommended by HN</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HackerTube：由 HN 推荐视频构成的 YouTube</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 12:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 488</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50319510'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007. The collection is available through hackertube.org. It turns years of community recommendations into a browsable video archive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。该视频集合可通过 hackertube.org 浏览。它将多年社区推荐整理成了一个可探索的视频档案。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50296470' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arkm.com/explorer/entity/21a94755-f604-45e5-9501-6542e23005e8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binance-hacker-1029 – $5.6B stolen from Binance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Binance 黑客案1029：从币安被盗5.6亿美元</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 14:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1750</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50296470'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches. On-chain tracing attempts to map the stolen funds as they move through wallets and mixers, showing how investigators pursue assets long after a hack. The lesson for builders is to strengthen custody security (multi-sig, cold storage) and incident response readiness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。链上追踪尝试绘出赃款去向，显示即便多年后仍有追踪的可能。对系统设计者的启示是加强资产托管安全（多签、冷存储）与快速事件响应能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50289418' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50289418' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re releasing the weights of Mistral Large 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>发布 Mistral Large 4 的权重</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 07:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1714</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 438</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50289418'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally. This move reduces reliance on hosted APIs and accelerates experimentation, but also brings licensing, safety, and governance considerations to the fore in open-weight AI projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。此举降低对 API 的依赖，促进实验，但也引发许可、安全与治理方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/cover-mistral-sovereign%20copie.webp' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/cover-mistral-sovereign%20copie.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50202285' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50202285' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Wikipedia: Wikipedia, but only for things a half hour walk from you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地维基：只收录你步行半小时内的事物</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 22, 2026 / 16:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1692</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50202285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing. This model foregrounds privacy and relevancy, but raises questions about coverage, updates, and shared standards across communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。此思路提升信息相关性，但也带来覆盖范围、更新频率和跨社区标准化等挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50242939' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/2130357/000162828026067168/anthropicpbc-sx1.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic S-1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的 S-1 招股书</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 06:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1628</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50242939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO. The filing will reveal business metrics, product roadmap, and risk factors as the company scales its AI safety-focused offerings. Investors will weigh profitability trajectory, capital needs, and competitive positioning in a crowded AI market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。文件将披露业务数据、产品路线图与风险因素，显示其在 AI 安全领域的扩张。投资者将评估盈利前景、资金需求以及在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中的定位。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276998' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50276998' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nullpt.rs/fingerprinting-bioweapons' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is this fingerprinting script creating bioweapons?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么这个指纹识别脚本在制造生物武器？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 10:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276998'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai. They then considered crawling the web, intercepting fingerprinting-related browser API calls, and mapping what was found. The title connects this investigation with scripts that appear to be creating bioweapons, though the supplied material does not provide further details.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。随后，作者考虑抓取网页、拦截与指纹识别相关的浏览器 API 调用，并绘制其分布图。标题将这项调查与似乎正在制造生物武器的脚本联系起来，但现有内容未提供更多细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nullpt.rs/posts/fingerprinting-bioweapons/canvas-gallery.jpg' data-full='https://nullpt.rs/posts/fingerprinting-bioweapons/canvas-gallery.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50271640' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50271640' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/apple.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts the compromised eMudhra certificates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 04:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 403</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50271640'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust. This highlights how supply chain security depends on up-to-date trust stores, and warrants prompt certificate revocation and rotation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。这凸显供应链安全对最新信任存储的依赖，应尽快撤销并轮换相关证书。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50272749' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50272749' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/ice-is-running-one-of-the-largest-anti-left-surveillance-operations-in-us-history/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evidence of a spying operation by ICE against left-wing social movements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE对左翼社会运动实施监视行动的证据</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 05:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 987</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50272749'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades. The findings raise questions about civil liberties, oversight, and the balance between border enforcement and political dissent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。相关证据推动对公民自由、监督机制以及边境执法与政治异议之间平衡的讨论。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50251344' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50251344' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/building-scientific-understanding-with-academic-communities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three new discoveries in mathematics, computer science, and physics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宣布数学、计算机科学与物理学三项新发现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 14:35 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。该裁决暴露出国家安全权力与公民自由之间的矛盾，也凸显对更大透明度和强有力监督的诉求。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软为约四分之一员工提供大规模自愿离职计划</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 08:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1018</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1049</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50245524'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs. The move fits into broader tech-sector efficiency drives as AI investments continue and macro pressures persist. It could reshape compensation bands, talent flow, and project prioritization across divisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。此举与科技行业的整体效率提升趋势相吻合，AI 投资与宏观压力并行推动成本优化。人力结构、人才流向和项目优先级可能因此调整。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在软件开发上应更具野心</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 14:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 892</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 769</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50225695'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps. Ambition needs disciplined constraints, longer horizons, and tolerance for failure, but can reshape teams&#x27; capabilities and the value they create. Expect practical guidance on bet-sizing, architecture, and collaboration to support bold bets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。实现这种野心需要清晰的约束、长期投入与对失败的容忍，进而改变团队能力与创造的价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/building-reasoning-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sebastian Raschka&#x27;s from-scratch video course on building reasoning models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sebastian Raschka 的从零开始构建推理模型的视频课程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 08:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 806</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50245138'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renowned ML educator Sebastian Raschka releases a hands-on video course teaching how to build reasoning models from scratch. The course emphasizes end-to-end design, debugging, and practical coding patterns to improve model reasoning capabilities. It provides a useful bridge between theory and implementation for AI researchers and engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名机器学习教育者 Sebastian Raschka 推出从零开始构建推理模型的视频课程，聚焦端到端设计与调试。课程强调实际编码模式，帮助提升模型推理能力。对 AI 研究者和工程师来说，这是理论到实现的有用桥梁。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50251661' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50251661' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Exponential Time Hypothesis Is False [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>指数时间假设不成立</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 14:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 792</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50251661'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory. If ETH fails, widespread implications follow for algorithm design and hardness results, potentially enabling faster solutions for some NP-hard problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。若 ETH 不再成立，算法设计与复杂性界限将被重新评估，某些 NP-hard 问题或将获得更高效的解法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9c8f408fa180ff619b1a10b59401aff152b396feccc1b9e6d6880ee408716cf5/openai/math' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9c8f408fa180ff619b1a10b59401aff152b396feccc1b9e6d6880ee408716cf5/openai/math' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50282454' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50282454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://data.stackexchange.com/stackoverflow/query/1926661#graph?t=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>StackOverflow now gets fewer than 1000 questions a month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月不到 1000 条问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 16:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 743</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 655</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50282454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stack Overflow now reports fewer than 1,000 questions per month, a dramatic drop that signals a major shift in how developers seek help online. The decline could reflect migration to other communities, changes in data collection, or platform strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stack Overflow 现每月提问不足一千条，显示开发者在线求助方式的显著转变。下降可能源于迁移到其他社区、数据收集变化或平台策略调整。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50320963' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50320963' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/italian-farmer-found-unknown-seeds-attic-with-nothing-lose-planted-them-result-biblical-180989644/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Italian farmer found some unknown seeds in his attic and planted them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>意大利农民在阁楼发现不明种子并将其种下</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 15:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50320963'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915. After he discarded the seeds in manure, several types of wheat unexpectedly sprouted. One variety grew about six feet tall and produced seven ears, unlike the single-ear commercial wheat Grossi knew.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。他把种子倒进肥料堆后，几种小麦竟意外发芽。其中一个品种长到约六英尺高，并结出七个麦穗，不同于格罗西熟悉的单穗商业小麦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/_-X8sWyx82CD0eX5ZxIF7dcS2X8=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1181x787:1182x788)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/37/95/3795362b-64a6-4d5f-b7df-67aa18192521/l2190910.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/_-X8sWyx82CD0eX5ZxIF7dcS2X8=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1181x787:1182x788)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/37/95/3795362b-64a6-4d5f-b7df-67aa18192521/l2190910.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50234474' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50234474' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://imaginationlibrary.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dolly Parton&#x27;s Imagination Library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多莉·帕頓的想象力圖書館</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 11:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 680</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50234474'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dolly Parton&#x27;s Imagination Library is a long-running charity that mails free children&#x27;s books to families to boost early literacy. The program scales through partnerships and donations and has shipped millions of books to children, spanning multiple countries and communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多莉·帕頓的想象力圖書館是一项长期慈善计划，通过合作与捐赠为家庭寄送免费儿童读物，促进幼年读写能力。该项目在多国开展，向儿童提供了大量书籍，受到教育界关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imaginationlibrary.com/ilData/usrFiles/2022/05/DPIL-Logo-195.png' data-full='https://imaginationlibrary.com/ilData/usrFiles/2022/05/DPIL-Logo-195.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50282838' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50282838' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://divagr.com/blog/qwenputer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwenputer: A functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU inside Qwen3.5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwenputer：在 Qwen3.5 内实现的确定性 RISC-V CPU</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 17:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50282838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwenputer reveals a functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU embedded inside Qwen3.5. The project demonstrates hardware-level determinism within a language-model environment, hinting at reproducible compute and new sandboxing possibilities for AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwenputer 展示了一个在 Qwen3.5 内部实现的可运行且确定性的 RISC-V CPU。此举表明可以在语言模型环境中进行硬件级别的确定性仿真，为可重复计算和 AI 系统的安全沙箱打开新可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://divagr.com/qwenputer/og.png' data-full='https://divagr.com/qwenputer/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50233779' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50233779' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lynchlab.net/llm-skin-cancer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 10-year-old CNN outperforms frontier LLMs for skin cancer diagnosis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十年前的 CNN 在皮肤癌诊断中超越前沿 LLM</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 10:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 665</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50233779'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decade-old CNN model reportedly outperforms frontier large language models on skin cancer diagnostic tasks, highlighting the strength of specialized vision systems for image-based medical problems. The result underscores that domain-specific models can outperform generic LLMs in certain tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款十年前的卷积神经网络在皮肤癌诊断任务中表现优于前沿的大型语言模型，凸显了专用视觉模型在图像类医疗问题上的优势。此结果提醒人们，在特定任务上，领域专用模型可能胜过通用的 LLM。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2gaqzonlm544h.cloudfront.net/skin-ai/samples/ISIC_7403025.jpg' data-full='https://d2gaqzonlm544h.cloudfront.net/skin-ai/samples/ISIC_7403025.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50305693' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50305693' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.signoz.io/p/ai-isnt-replacing-sres-its-deskilling' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI isn&#x27;t replacing SREs, it&#x27;s deskilling them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 没有取代 SRE，而是在让他们技能退化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 08:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 662</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 482</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50305693'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece asks whether engineers become less capable when AI handles 95% of incident response, leaving humans with only the most difficult 5%. It argues that a human should remain responsible for carrying out actions, citing a historical example from the US Navy. In novel incidents, the author describes using AI alongside human observation and reasoning to narrow down possible root causes. The result is presented as continued human participation rather than complete replacement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出疑问：如果 AI 处理了95%的事故响应，人类只面对最关键的5%，工程师是否会因此变得不那么熟练。文章援引美国海军的历史案例，主张始终应由人类负责执行操作。面对新型事故时，作者会结合自身观察和推理使用 AI，以逐步排除可能的根因。文中描述的是人机协作，而不是完全取代人类。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hgvq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F530d847f-26cb-4ddd-a6de-875cbbaf8fab_6233x4167.webp' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hgvq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F530d847f-26cb-4ddd-a6de-875cbbaf8fab_6233x4167.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50257325' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50257325' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oioannou.com/blog/ai-promised-less-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI promised less work. I&#x27;ve never worked more</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI承诺减轻工作量，但我却工作得更多</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 04:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 649</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50257325'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI promised to reduce our workload, but real-world use has often done the opposite. The piece traces how integrating AI into daily work creates new bottlenecks—tool fragmentation, data wrangling, and constant monitoring—so total effort climbs rather than falls. For technologists, the takeaway is to redesign workflows to balance human oversight with AI, rather than chasing blind efficiency gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 原本承诺能减轻工作量，现实使用却让工作量进一步增加。把 AI 融入日常工作带来工具碎片化、数据整理和持续监控等新负担，导致总投入上升。对技术人员而言，关键在于重新设计工作流，平衡人机协同，而不是盲目追求表面的效率提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://oioannou.com/img/blog-by-orestis.jpg' data-full='https://oioannou.com/img/blog-by-orestis.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50319143' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50319143' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TCP Use-After-Free: CVE-2026-98432 / CVSS 9.8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TCP 释放后使用：CVE-2026-98432，CVSS 9.8</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 12:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319143' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319143' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50319143'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer. Stable kernels fixed it in October 2026, with patches for versions including 5.10.272 through 7.3, but the flaw dates back to Linux 4.4. At the reported time, Ubuntu still marked every release as vulnerable, while Red Hat listed RHEL 8, 9, and 10 as affected without a fix.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。稳定版内核已于 2026 年 10 月修复，补丁覆盖 5.10.272 至 7.3 等版本，但该缺陷可追溯至 Linux 4.4。报道所述时间点，Ubuntu 仍将所有版本标为易受攻击，Red Hat 也列出受影响的 RHEL 8、9 和 10，尚未提供修复。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf/opengraph-image' data-full='https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf/opengraph-image' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50248124' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50248124' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-6-1-terra/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-6.1 Terra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-6.1 Terra</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 11:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 425</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50248124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI releases GPT-6.1 Terra, continuing the model family with improvements in reasoning, safety controls, and developer tooling. The update affects how teams deploy and integrate AI into products, with potential API changes and new capabilities for multi-model orchestration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布 GPT-6.1 Terra，延续模型家族并在推理能力、安全控制和开发者工具方面带来改进。这一更新将影响团队在产品中部署与集成 AI 的方式，可能伴随 API 变更与多模型编排的新功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.openai.com/mdx-assets/71f084db-a08b-48c5-b96e-5fbdc21a1a00/terra-art-card-16x9.0394724b.png' data-full='https://cdn.openai.com/mdx-assets/71f084db-a08b-48c5-b96e-5fbdc21a1a00/terra-art-card-16x9.0394724b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276940' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50276940' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/macbook-pro/proled-xdr-display/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Pro with PROLED XDR Display and Touch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>配备 PROLED XDR 显示屏和触控的 MacBook Pro</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 10:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 607</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1052</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276940'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils a MacBook Pro featuring a new PROLED XDR display and touch input, promising vibrant color, high brightness, and responsive interaction. The move signals a push toward higher-end display tech and more interactive laptops.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出搭载全新 PROLED XDR 显示屏与触控输入的 MacBook Pro，承诺带来更丰富的色彩、极高的亮度与灵敏的交互体验，标志着对高端显示技术与触控交互的进一步推进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50303172' data-tags='Security,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50303172' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/the-dollar63-billion-binance-hack-was-pulled-off-in-just-20-minutes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The $6.3B Binance hack was pulled off in just 20 minutes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>价值 63 亿美元的币安黑客攻击仅用时 20 分钟</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 05:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50303172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 585</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50303172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 438</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50303172'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers siphoned roughly $6.3 billion in cryptocurrency from wallets belonging to Binance across multiple blockchains in about 20 minutes. Binance suspended deposits and withdrawals and began investigating, while the exchange and other crypto firms said they had frozen around half of the stolen funds. Much of the remaining cryptocurrency may not be recoverable through freezing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客在约 20 分钟内从币安所属的钱包中跨多个区块链转走了约 63 亿美元的加密货币。币安暂停了存款和提现并展开调查；该交易所及其他加密行业公司称，已冻结约一半被盗资金。剩余的大部分加密货币可能无法通过冻结追回。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae463bdc091c8b8d1ec019e/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/102026-Binance%20Hacked.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae463bdc091c8b8d1ec019e/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/102026-Binance%20Hacked.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50208960' data-tags='Education,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50208960' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vertex.degree/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Start Doing Graph Theory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现在就开始学习图论</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 06:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 582</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50208960'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical nudge to dive into graph theory, highlighting how graphs underpin networks, algorithms, and data structures. The piece outlines intuitions, common pitfalls, and approachable starting points—paths, connectivity, flows—to build powerful problem-solving habits. Grasping graph concepts can sharpen software design, optimization, and security analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章鼓励直接入门图论，强调图在网络、算法与数据结构中的核心作用。给出直观要点与易上手的起始主题（如路经、连通性、流等），以培养强大的问题解决思维。掌握图论对软件设计、优化与安全分析都有帮助。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vertex.degree/images/link.png' data-full='https://vertex.degree/images/link.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50260814' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stanforddaily.com/2026/10/27/professors-books-pirated-to-train-claude/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic trained Claude on illegally obtained Stanford professors&#x27; books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 在 Claude 的训练中使用了非法获得的斯坦福教授书籍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 08:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50260814'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic reportedly trained Claude on pirated books from Stanford professors, raising ethics concerns and potential legal risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Anthropic 使用了从斯坦福教授处非法获取的书籍来训练 Claude，这引发伦理担忧并可能带来法律风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stanforddaily.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Green-Library.png' data-full='https://stanforddaily.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Green-Library.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50257419' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50257419' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102102/0c00e4d550f25f8c/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python&#x27;s experimental JIT to be removed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 实验性 JIT 将被移除</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 04:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50257419'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Python core developers plan to remove the experimental JIT after evaluating its benefits and maintenance burden. The decision suggests Python will focus on language-level optimizations and alternative runtimes, while users needing JIT performance should rely on projects like PyPy or other tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CPython 的实验性 JIT 将被移除，原因在于实际收益不足以覆盖维护成本。这意味着 Python 将继续以解释器层优化为主，用户若需要 JIT 性能可转向 PyPy 等替代实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50233929' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://talkweb.eu/openweb/4558/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I refuse to accept a censored Internet. Join Me at FOSDEM 27</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我拒绝接受被审查的互联网，加入我在 FOSDEM 27</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 10:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 280</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50233929'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A manifesto-style appeal to defend an uncensored Internet, inviting readers to join a talk at FOSDEM 27. The message centers on preserving open standards and resisting censorship amid growing platform control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以宣言体撰写，倡导捍卫未被审查的互联网，并邀请读者参加 FOSDEM 27 的演讲。信息聚焦于保护开放标准、抗拒审查，以及在平台日益集中的环境中的自由与协作。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50245831' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://css-tricks.com/css-tricks-has-a-new-owner/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CSS-Tricks has a new owner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS-Tricks 迎来新所有者</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 08:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50245831'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSS-Tricks, the long-running web design site, has changed ownership, hinting at possible shifts in editorial voice, monetization, or product strategy. The buyer’s priorities may influence tutorials, paid content, and community engagement. Existing readers will watch for changes in tone and quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>长期运营的网页设计站点 CSS-Tricks 完成易主，可能带来编辑风格、商业化或产品策略的变化。买家的重点将影响教程、付费内容和社区互动。读者将关注语气、内容质量的变化。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50284255' data-tags='Privacy,Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50284255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code regularly sends each background agent’s transcript to its model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 会定期将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 20:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50284255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers. This telemetry behavior highlights how data sharing can impact confidentiality in code tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。此类遥测行为凸显了代码工具在数据共享方面的潜在风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50248055' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50248055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.enlyth.com/posts/oldest-windows-gui-app-win-11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oldest GUI application that still runs on Windows 11</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仍在 Windows 11 上运行的最古老 GUI 应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 10:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50248055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A legacy GUI application still running on Windows 11 demonstrates the platform&#x27;s backward compatibility strengths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款极早的 GUI 应用仍能在 Windows 11 上正常运行，展示了该平台对旧软件的向后兼容性。文章讨论了仿真与兼容性机制如何让遗留应用继续可用，并对 IT 战略和长期软件支持有何影响。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50264853' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.brangus.io/survival-llm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Survival LLM: AI assistant and emergency library that runs on your own computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Survival LLM：可在本地运行的 AI 助手与应急知识库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50264853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50264853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50264853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Survival LLM provides an AI assistant and an offline knowledge library that runs entirely on your own computer. This design emphasizes privacy, resilience, and disaster-scenario readiness, letting teams access critical information without cloud dependence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Survival LLM 提供一个可在本地运行的 AI 助手和应急知识库，完全在本地计算机上运作。此设计强调隐私与韧性，支持在没有云端的情况下访问关键信息，提升灾害情景下的可用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.brangus.io/survival-llm/social-card.png' data-full='https://www.brangus.io/survival-llm/social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50215997' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50215997' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20261019-birdwatching-rewires-the-brain-and-slows-cognitive-decline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Birdwatching rewires the brain and slows cognitive decline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>观鸟能重新塑造大脑并减缓认知衰退</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 15:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50215997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50215997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50215997'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Watching birds engages attention and memory networks, promoting neuroplasticity in aging brains. Emerging evidence suggests nature-based, cognitively engaging hobbies may help slow cognitive decline and support mental well-being.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观鸟能激活大脑的注意力与记忆网络，促进老年人群的神经可塑性。日益增多的证据表明，参与自然和认知挑战性的爱好可能有助于减缓认知衰退并提升心理健康。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pj6qr2.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pj6qr2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50234232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/10/01/oregon-police-misconduct-public-records-cost-fee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oregon Police Are Charging a Local News Outlet $2.5M for Records About One Cop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>俄勒冈州警方就一名警察的记录向当地新闻机构索取250万美元</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 11:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50234232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public records fees escalate when a local news outlet is charged $2.5 million for records about a single officer, raising concerns about transparency, access to information, and the use of exorbitant fees to suppress reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家本地新闻机构被要求就一名警察的记录支付250万美元的费用，此举引发对政府透明度、信息获取权及以高额费用压制报道的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Screenshot-2026-10-01-at-4.14.38-PM-e1790885738599.png?fit=1436%2C718&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' data-full='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Screenshot-2026-10-01-at-4.14.38-PM-e1790885738599.png?fit=1436%2C718&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.circuithub.com/post/unveiling-the-grid' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Grid, a factory-scale robot in Massachusetts that builds PCBs on demand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>The Grid：马萨诸塞州的工厂级机器人按需制造 PCB</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 06:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50243060'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a factory-scale robotic system that fabricates PCBs on demand, signaling progress toward distributed, on‑demand manufacturing. It raises questions about throughput, quality control, and the economics of localized PCB production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一套支持按需制造 PCB 的工厂级机器人系统，显示出分布式、按需生产的潜力。引发对产能、质控和本地化 PCB 生产经济性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5e4f3506e94717243b3bdc56/6adf37db9c9a7b067b1675e7_6adf37a9ebc649853e42585e_the-grid-launch-og.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5e4f3506e94717243b3bdc56/6adf37db9c9a7b067b1675e7_6adf37a9ebc649853e42585e_the-grid-launch-og.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://carmelitegothic.com/cnc-stone-carving/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CNC Stone Carving</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50274409'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CNC stone carving blends automation with traditional craftsmanship, enabling precise, repeatable stonework for architecture and sculpture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CNC 石材雕刻将自动化与传统工艺结合，实现建筑与雕塑领域的高精度、可重复加工。内容涵盖工作流、工具选型以及材料要点，以提升雕刻质量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/live/brazil-election-live-lula-bolsonaro-face-off-presidential-runoff-2026-10-25/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lula wins election after final votes tallied</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 17:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 641</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237485'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lula wins Brazil&#x27;s runoff after final tallies, setting the stage for policy shifts in areas like economy and tech regulation. The result reins in market expectations and signals continuity in Brazil&#x27;s political trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卢拉在最终计票后赢得总统大选，预计将对经济与数字治理等领域的政策产生影响。此次结果将稳定市场预期，并体现巴西政治走向的延续性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.technologyreview.com/2026/10/20/1145399/emacs-turns-50/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emacs Turns 50</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Emacs迎来50周年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 06:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50273171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 507</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50273171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50273171'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emacs turns 50, celebrating half a century of extensible, Lisp-based text editing that has influenced code editors and development workflows. Despite aging interfaces, its ecosystem endures through deep customization and a dedicated user base.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Emacs迎来50周年，庆祝这款基于 Lisp 的可扩展文本编辑器在代码编辑和开发工作流中的长期影响力。尽管界面相对陈旧，其通过深度定制和忠实用户群体仍保持生态活力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wp.technologyreview.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/cover-mockup-thumb.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://wp.technologyreview.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/cover-mockup-thumb.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50290177' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/bliki/LLM-Voice.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM Voice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型的语音交互</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 07:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 506</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50290177'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLM Voice examines how language models function in voice-based interfaces, separate from text prompts, emphasizing the need to address speech recognition, latency, and user expectations. It argues for treating voice as a distinct modality with tailored UX and safety considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LLM 在语音场景中的交互方式需要和文本输入不同的设计原则。本文强调处理语音识别、延迟和用户预期的重要性，并提出面向语音的 UX 和安全性设计要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/logo-sq.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/logo-sq.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20261023-how-solar-became-bigger-than-nuclear' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solar power is becoming bigger than nuclear</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 08:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 501</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 691</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50245385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar power is growing to exceed nuclear in capacity, marking a major shift in the energy landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>太阳能在容量方面正超越核电，成为更大的能源来源，标志着能源结构的重大转变。文章讨论成本下降、部署加速以及并网挑战，对科技基础设施和气候目标影响深远。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pjn1gz.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pjn1gz.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50257326' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/wikipedia-wikiproject-ai-writing-cleanup' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia&#x27;s Cleanup of AI-Generated Content</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维基百科对 AI 生成内容的清理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 04:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50257326'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wikipedia launches a cleanup initiative aimed at AI-generated edits, emphasizing provenance and reliable sourcing. The move highlights governance questions for large collaborative knowledge projects as AI-assisted contributions grow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维基百科启动针对 AI 生成内容的清理行动，强调来源证明与可靠的引用。随着 AI 助力的投稿增多，这项举措凸显了大型协作知识库在治理方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/surreal-collage-of-a-womans-face-with-geometric-shapes-pencil-and-wikipedia-globe.png?id=68486571&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C167%2C0%2C168' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/surreal-collage-of-a-womans-face-with-geometric-shapes-pencil-and-wikipedia-globe.png?id=68486571&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C167%2C0%2C168' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
