<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50273396' data-tags='Legal,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50273396' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://religionnews.com/2026/10/26/in-a-first-a-humanist-granted-a-religious-exemption-from-using-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kentucky man becomes the first humanist granted religious exemption from AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肯塔基男子成首位获批就使用AI申请宗教豁免的人文主义者</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 06:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50273396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50273396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50273396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Kentucky humanist obtained a religious exemption to use AI, marking a notable legal milestone in how religious beliefs intersect with workplace AI policies. The decision signals how courts and regulators may rethink AI usage rules in diverse belief systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>肯塔基一位人文主义者获得了使用 AI 的宗教豁免，这是宗教信仰与工作场所 AI 使用规则交叉的一个重要里程碑。此举可能推动对 AI 使用政策在不同信仰体系中的再考量。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50305025' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50305025' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.northflank.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Northflank Breached</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Northflank 遭到入侵</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 08:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 477</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50305025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Northflank says secret rotation for MySQL and Redis is now available to all customers and advises customers to rotate secrets if they have not already. It has also enabled audit logs for all customers, including free users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Northflank 表示，所有客户现在都可以轮换 MySQL 和 Redis 密钥，并建议尚未操作的客户立即轮换。该平台还为包括免费用户在内的所有客户启用了审计日志。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://northflank.instatus.com/api/og' data-full='https://northflank.instatus.com/api/og' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50256985' data-tags='Gaming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50256985' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://open3dlab.com/news/roblox-downloaded-145-gb-of-models-from-our-sites/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roblox downloaded 145GB models from us (including adult content) without asking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Roblox 未经授权从我们的网站下载了145GB模型（含成人内容）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 03:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50256985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 477</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50256985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 271</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50256985'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Roblox clients downloaded 145GB of user-uploaded 3D models without permission, including adult content. This underscores challenges around asset provenance and moderation on large user-generated platforms, and highlights the need for stronger safeguards and provenance tracking in download flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Roblox 客户端未获同意便下载了约145GB的用户上传3D模型，其中还包含成人内容。这突显了大规模用户生成内容平台在资产溯源和分发治理方面的难题，需要加强下载保护与溯源机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thumbcdn.open3dlab.com/newsitem/3200w.webp' data-full='https://thumbcdn.open3dlab.com/newsitem/3200w.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50240922' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50240922' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blocks.manifold3d.ch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switzerland. Block by block. At 1:1 scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士：逐块构建，1:1 尺度</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 01:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50240922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 473</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50240922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50240922'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Block-by-block construction achieves a life-size replica of Switzerland, highlighting how modular fabrication can translate a complex geography into a tangible model. The project invites readers to consider scale, precision, and how accessible tooling could democratize large physical builds. It showcases how digital design and modular blocks enable collaborative, hands-on exploration of a country.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>逐块构建实现对瑞士的1:1比例实物复刻，展示了模块化制造如何将复杂地形转化为可触及的模型。这个项目促使人们思考尺度与精度，以及可访问工具如何让大规模实体搭建更具参与性。它展示了数字设计与模块化积木如何推动协作式、动手的国家探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blocks.manifold3d.ch/bilder/held.jpg' data-full='https://blocks.manifold3d.ch/bilder/held.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50287919' data-tags='Business,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50287919' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://about.doordash.com/en-us/news/doordash-to-acquire-instacart' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DoorDash to acquire Instacart</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DoorDash 将收购 Instacart</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 05:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 471</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 632</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50287919'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DoorDash announced plans to acquire Instacart, creating a larger player in on-demand grocery delivery. The merger could reshape competition, pricing, and last-mile logistics, while triggering antitrust scrutiny and integration challenges for both platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DoorDash 宣布收购 Instacart，将打造更强的即时送货生态。合并可能改变市场竞争格局、定价与末端配送效率，同时面临潜在的反垄断审查和整合挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/trvmqu12jq2l/5qfYy7VGputct2yoKgzgj6/35e4e5767aaf32b6f868c2a89de7c89d/DoorDash_to_Acquire_Instacart_-_newsroom.png' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/trvmqu12jq2l/5qfYy7VGputct2yoKgzgj6/35e4e5767aaf32b6f868c2a89de7c89d/DoorDash_to_Acquire_Instacart_-_newsroom.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50255298' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50255298' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://j11g.com/the-mythical-man-month-in-the-age-of-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Mythical Man-Month in the Age of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI时代的人月神话</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 23:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50255298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 471</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50255298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50255298'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The discussion applies Brooks’ classic Man-Month ideas to AI-enabled work, arguing that AI tools can boost productivity but don’t eliminate coordination, architecture, or integration costs. Planning must account for coupling, learning curves, and human-in-the-loop decisions rather than assuming AI shortens all timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将《人月神话》理念应用于 AI 助力的工作，认为 AI 能提升生产力但无法消除协作、架构与集成成本。 项目估算应考虑耦合、学习曲线以及人机协作的决策，而非假设 AI 能缩短所有周期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://j11g.com/content/images/cern-resized.jpg' data-full='https://j11g.com/content/images/cern-resized.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50280864' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50280864' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/massive-leak-exposes-41000-homeland-security-workers-including-undercover-ice-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leak Exposes 41,000 Homeland Security Workers–Including Undercover ICE Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泄露事件曝光4.1万名国土安全工作人员，包括卧底 ICE 特工</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 14:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50280864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 461</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50280864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50280864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A leak exposed details of more than 41,000 employees and contractors at the US Department of Homeland Security and seven component agencies. The records include email addresses, phone numbers, office locations, references to internal systems, and sensitive information about undercover and surveillance programs. The data was last updated on August 19, 2026, and was previewed by Distributed Denial of Secrets and researcher-journalist maia arson crimew. The records reportedly came from a group calling itself the Department of Peace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次数据泄露曝光了美国国土安全部及其七个下属机构超过4.1万名员工和承包商的信息。记录包括电子邮件地址、电话号码、办公地点、内部系统信息，以及有关卧底和监控项目的敏感内容。数据最后更新于2026年8月19日，由 Distributed Denial of Secrets 和研究员兼记者 maia arson crimew 预览。两者称数据来自一个自称“和平部”的组织。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae126cfb5fdd6fa181bb24f/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Massive%20Leak%20Exposes%2041,000%20DHS%20Employees_v1.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae126cfb5fdd6fa181bb24f/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Massive%20Leak%20Exposes%2041,000%20DHS%20Employees_v1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50291054' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50291054' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sidbala.com/h-264-is-magic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>H.264 Is Magic (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>H.264 的魔力（2016）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 08:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50291054'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>H.264 Is Magic (2016) analyzes why the H.264 video codec became a de-facto standard, enabling efficient compression, broad hardware support, and widespread adoption. It discusses licensing realities that helped lock in the standard and powered decades of streaming and video apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>H.264 的魔力（2016）解析为何 H.264 成为事实标准、实现高效压缩与广泛的硬件支持。文章还讨论了授权环境如何将该标准锁定并推动多年来的流媒体与视频应用发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/55/d9/55d91d94-f430-41c7-b2da-d3e4e9822c0b/content/images/size/w1200/2016/11/Artboard.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/55/d9/55d91d94-f430-41c7-b2da-d3e4e9822c0b/content/images/size/w1200/2016/11/Artboard.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50268380' data-tags='Programming,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50268380' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lexifina.com/blog/git-for-the-us-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git for the US Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Git 管理美国法典</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 18:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50268380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 445</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50268380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50268380'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Versioning the US Code with Git shows how large legal texts can be edited transparently and auditable over time. The piece weighs practical challenges—structure, numbering, and edition standards—and points to developer-style workflows (PRs, diffs) as a path to more collaborative lawmaking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用 Git 对美国法典进行版本控制展示了大型法文本如何实现可追溯、透明的编辑过程。文章评估了结构、编号和版本标准等实际挑战，并探讨了采用开发者工作流（合并请求、变更对比）以促进协作式修订的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lexifina.com/social-preview-v3.png?v=20261018' data-full='https://lexifina.com/social-preview-v3.png?v=20261018' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50319358' data-tags='AI,Legal,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50319358' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jiahaoshao1/status/2116475922190922088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic blocks users for anything touching training larger models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 屏蔽任何涉及训练更大模型的用户</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 12:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 435</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 245</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50319358'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user says Anthropic blocked their account after flagging work involving reinforcement fine-tuning, supervised fine-tuning, or training larger models as “training a competitor.” The user says Claude only helped submit jobs and analyze results, while the rollouts came from an open-source model. They also report being downgraded from Opus 5.5.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名用户称，Anthropic 将其涉及强化微调、监督微调或训练更大模型的工作标记为“训练竞争对手”，并屏蔽了其账户。该用户表示，Claude 只帮助提交任务和分析结果，实际生成过程使用的是开源模型。用户还称自己的账户被降级，无法继续使用 Opus 5.5。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1983126868095315969/28wHeS_J_400x400.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1983126868095315969/28wHeS_J_400x400.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50236102' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50236102' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://minimaxir.com/2026/10/cheap-llms/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI and Anthropic cut their LLM prices by up to 80% within a few months. Why?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与 Anthropic 在数月内大幅降价：背后的原因</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 14:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50236102'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI and Anthropic slashed LLM prices by up to 80% in a matter of months as competition and cost efficiencies compress margins. The move widens access for developers and startups, but profitability pressures and continued innovation costs remain a concern.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 与 Anthropic 在数月内将大模型价格降幅高达80%，竞争加剧与成本效率提高压缩利润空间。降价扩大了开发者与初创公司的使用机会，但盈利压力与持续创新成本仍需关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://minimaxir.com/2026/10/cheap-llms/vercel.png' data-full='https://minimaxir.com/2026/10/cheap-llms/vercel.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50238088' data-tags='AI,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50238088' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/systems/2026/10/22/ibm-ceo-says-open-weight-models-are-now-irresistibly-good-and-cheap-on-prem/5304640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IBM CEO says open weight models are now good and cheap on-prem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IBM 首席执行官称开源权重模型现在在本地部署更好且更便宜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 18:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50238088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50238088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50238088'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IBM&#x27;s CEO argues that open-weight models are now viable and affordable to deploy on-prem, addressing data privacy and latency concerns for enterprises. The stance signals a shift away from cloud-only AI toward private, controllable deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IBM CEO 指出开源权重模型现在在本地部署可行且成本更低，能解决企业数据隐私与延迟等顾虑。这表明企业AI可能从云端转向更可控的本地部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/4091853.jpg?imageId=4091853&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/4091853.jpg?imageId=4091853&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50261352' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils the 14-inch MacBook Pro with the M6, highlighting performance gains, display improvements, and efficiency, continuing the high-end laptop lineup.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果发布搭载 M6 的 14 吋 MacBook Pro，强调性能提升、显示效果与能效优化，丰富了高端笔记本产品线。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50296486' data-tags='AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50296486' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://labs.ycombinator.com/projects/games/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We ran AI agent swarms in Minecraft and Mindustry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Minecraft 与 Mindustry 中测试 AI 代理群</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 14:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 412</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50296486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers ran AI agent swarms in Minecraft and Mindustry to study emergent coordination at scale. The experiments illustrate how simple agents, when put in shared environments, can exhibit complex group behaviors, informing the design of scalable multi-agent systems and game AI safety. The work provides hands-on demos for researchers building autonomous agents in synthetic worlds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Minecraft 与 Mindustry 中测试AI代理群，研究群体协作与尺度效应。实验表明，简单代理在共享环境中可涌现出复杂的协同行为，为多智能体系统和游戏AI安全提供直观范例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://labs.ycombinator.com/og.png' data-full='https://labs.ycombinator.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50234796' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50234796' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/meet-the-2026-winner-of-nikons-small-world-photomicrography-contest/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ink droplets in motion snag top prize in Nikon Small World contest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>墨滴在运动中夺冠：尼康 Small World 摄影比赛冠军</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 11:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50234796'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The winner captures ink droplets in motion using high-speed macro photography, revealing striking fluid dynamics at tiny scales. The entry demonstrates how microphotography can make invisible phenomena visually compelling and showcases the technical skill behind Nikon&#x27;s Small World contest.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该作品通过高速微距技术捕捉墨滴在运动中的瞬间，展现肉眼看不见的流体动力细节。它证明微观摄影不仅具备科学价值，也能以强烈的视觉冲击力讲述科学现象，同时体现参赛作品的创意与技术水平。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/nikonTOP-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/nikonTOP-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50238708' data-tags='Show HN,Web,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50238708' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://titleart.dvy.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: TitleArt – Make text like it&#x27;s 1997</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: TitleArt——让文本重现1997年的风格</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 20:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50238708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50238708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50238708'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TitleArt is a tiny project that renders text in retro 1990s typography. It lets developers quickly mock up headings with nostalgic styling without heavy assets. It illustrates how simple CSS and design cues can evoke a specific era without a full design system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TitleArt 是一个小型项目，能够将文本渲染为1990年代风格的排版。它让开发者在没有繁重依赖的情况下快速为标题添加怀旧效果。此举展示了无需完整设计系统就能营造特定时代感的简洁美学。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://titleart.dvy.io/social-preview.png' data-full='https://titleart.dvy.io/social-preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50241664' data-tags='Web,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50241664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brennenputh.me/blog/printable-websites/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Websites Should Be Printable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网页应可打印</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 03:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50241664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50241664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50241664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Web pages should offer printer-friendly versions that preserve core content while removing navigation and clutter. Printer-focused CSS enables clean, readable copies for receipts, manuals, or offline reading. Embracing print-friendly design improves accessibility and productivity across devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>网页应提供可打印版本，在保持核心内容的同时去除导航与多余元素。打印专用的 CSS 能让复制品整洁易读，适用于收据、手册或离线阅读。采用可打印设计有助于提升跨设备的可访问性与工作效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://brennenputh.me/print_icon.svg' data-full='https://brennenputh.me/print_icon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50320995' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50320995' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/guregodevo/memdoor/tree/main/examples/workflows/team-solve' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memdoor: A team of 5 Qwen3.8-27B models beats Fable 5 on LiveCodeBench Hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Memdoor：五个 Qwen3.8-27B 模型在 LiveCodeBench Hard 上击败 Fable 5</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 15:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50320995'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker. Each model starts with a fresh context and communicates only through files, while an external script determines whether solutions pass. The authors reproduced the method using Qwen3.8-27B, eight YAML files, their prompts, and a 128K response limit.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。每个模型都从全新的上下文开始，仅通过磁盘文件通信，并由外部脚本判断解答是否通过。作者使用 Qwen3.8-27B、八个 YAML 文件、自定义提示词和 128K 回复上限复现了这一方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/90cac1f11b2ff0b579e46fabadb925454a173bd0f411b81a2263383746cfc66d/guregodevo/memdoor' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/90cac1f11b2ff0b579e46fabadb925454a173bd0f411b81a2263383746cfc66d/guregodevo/memdoor' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50288867' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50288867' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2014/11/16/magazine/what-an-uncensored-letter-to-mlk-reveals.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>‘You Are Done’: The letter sent to King by the F.B.I (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你完了：FBI 致马丁·路德·金的信（2014）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 06:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50288867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50288867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 321</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50288867'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The uncensored FBI letter to Martin Luther King Jr. from the 1960s reveals coercive surveillance tactics and personal pressure used to undermine civil rights leadership. The document provides a stark view of how state power interacted with dissent, and its release invites renewed scrutiny of civil liberties in government overreach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这封未删改的 FBI 信件揭示了上世纪60年代对马丁·路德·金的监控与施压。信件展示了政府权力如何试图压制民权领袖的声音，引发对公民自由与政府越权行为的再关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50264857' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50264857' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/ssh-investigation-update' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailscale SSH: Investigation and remediation update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailscale SSH：调查进展与整改更新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50264857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50264857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50264857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale has published an investigation update on a security incident affecting its SSH feature. The update outlines the root cause, affected components, and remediation steps, including patches and credential rotation. The incident underscores the importance of timely updates and access-control hygiene in zero-trust environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale 已发布关于影响其 SSH 功能的安全事件调查更新。更新概述了根本原因、受影响的组件与整改措施，包括补丁与凭证轮换。此事件凸显在零信任环境中及时更新和访问控制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tailscale.com/files/images/og-image.png' data-full='https://tailscale.com/files/images/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50261819' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50261819' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/police-are-using-ai-to-pull-over-drivers-for-suspicious-behavior-2000831220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Police are using AI to pull over drivers for &#x27;suspicious&#x27; behavior</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警方正在使用 AI 以可疑行为拦车</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 09:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50261819'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Law enforcement agencies are deploying AI systems to flag suspicious driving patterns and pull people over. The approach raises civil liberties concerns, potential bias, and questions about accountability and accuracy in real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>执法机构正在部署 AI 系统以标记可疑的驾驶模式并拦下车辆。这种做法引发公民自由、潜在偏见以及现实应用中的问责与准确性等问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50309647' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50309647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bostonglobe.com/2026/10/29/metro/patrick-stewart-picard-star-trek-the-next-generation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Through Captain Jean-Luc Picard, Patrick Stewart taught us the measure of a man</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过让·吕克·皮卡尔舰长，帕特里克·斯图尔特教会我们何谓大丈夫</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 13:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50309647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50309647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50309647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patrick Stewart’s portrayal of Captain Jean-Luc Picard presented a complex character who strived to do what was right. The character influenced a generation of young viewers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕特里克·斯图尔特饰演的让·吕克·皮卡尔舰长，是一个努力做正确之事的复杂人物。这个角色影响了一代年轻观众。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bostonglobe-prod.cdn.arcpublishing.com/resizer/v2/CGTKMVCJ3II6DM4QQY75UQKYRQ.jpg?auth=1665db2d602a73cebcb708de2cff81cdafb1dff34e84e5e1b3f511472b91079a&amp;width=1440' data-full='https://bostonglobe-prod.cdn.arcpublishing.com/resizer/v2/CGTKMVCJ3II6DM4QQY75UQKYRQ.jpg?auth=1665db2d602a73cebcb708de2cff81cdafb1dff34e84e5e1b3f511472b91079a&amp;width=1440' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50305637' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50305637' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Florilegium: Procedural Flowers in LaTeX</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Florilegium：用 LaTeX 生成程序化花卉装饰</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 08:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50305637'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua. It combines hand-authored primitives, randomized parameters, and a procedural layout that fills unused space. The creator says the project produces a similar look to a referenced design without trying to replicate it, and used Claude during development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。它结合手工编写的基本图形、随机参数和用于填充空白区域的程序化布局。作者表示，该项目形成了与某个参考设计相似的效果，但并未试图复制原作，开发过程中使用了 Claude。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e6ea3163fcfb443072b7630c9cbac9767a56ce80e6fb20963d217e704af2a020/kevinraymond/florilegium' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e6ea3163fcfb443072b7630c9cbac9767a56ce80e6fb20963d217e704af2a020/kevinraymond/florilegium' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50291945' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50291945' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kadoa.com/companies/insights/google-search-below-half' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Search is now less than half of Alphabet’s revenue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌搜索营收占比跌破一半，成 Alphabet 新挑战</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 09:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 376</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 319</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50291945'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alphabet&#x27;s core search business now accounts for less than half of its revenue, underscoring a broader shift toward cloud, YouTube, and other bets. The trend highlights how platform diversification affects investor sentiment and strategic priorities for large tech companies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Alphabet 的核心搜索业务如今贡献不足一半的营收，显示云计算、YouTube 等新领域的增长分担作用日益增大。此趋势影响投资者情绪与大型科技公司未来的战略重点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.kadoa.com/companies/og/insights/google-search-below-half.png' data-full='https://www.kadoa.com/companies/og/insights/google-search-below-half.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/imac/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New iMac with M6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>搭载 M6 的新款 iMac</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50258538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50258538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 471</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50258538'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils a refreshed iMac featuring the M6 chip, promising improved CPU/GPU performance and energy efficiency. The update also brings display and port refinements, signaling Apple&#x27;s continued focus on the all-in-one desktop experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出搭载 M6 芯片的新款 iMac，承诺在 CPU/GPU 性能与能效方面带来显著提升。此次更新还对显示与接口进行改进，体现苹果对一体机的持续关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50314699' data-tags='Security,Privacy,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50314699' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.parameter.ai/labs/we-found-a-gap-at-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do the math: How we took over ChatGPT accounts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们接管了 ChatGPT 账户</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 03:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50314699'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers bypassed ChatGPT’s sandbox and content security policy to take over accounts by sending victims a link. In October 2026, they found that a public ChatGPT share could be turned into attacker-controlled JavaScript running inside the signed-in session of anyone who opened it. An attacker could publish a malicious conversation through ChatGPT’s normal sharing feature, and the payload would execute on chatgpt.com with access to the victim’s authenticated web session.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员绕过了 ChatGPT 的沙箱和内容安全策略，只需向受害者发送链接即可接管账户。2026 年 10 月，他们发现可以将普通的 ChatGPT 公开分享转换为攻击者控制的 JavaScript，并在打开链接者的登录会话中运行。攻击者可以通过 ChatGPT 的正常分享功能发布恶意对话，载荷随后会以 chatgpt.com 的身份执行，并获得受害者已认证的网页会话权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.parameter.ai/opengraph-image.png' data-full='https://www.parameter.ai/opengraph-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50261589' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/amazon-ai-division-failure-nova' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon Is Gutting Its AI Division After Sustained Failure</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50261589'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon is downsizing its AI division after sustained underperformance, signaling a strategic rethink and resource reallocation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊在持续表现不佳后缩减 AI 部门，标志着其策略性调整与资源再分配。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50312756' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50312756' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50312756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Scribd Breached</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN：Scribd 遭到入侵</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 19:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50312756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 346</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50312756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50312756'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scribd notified users of a security incident in which an unauthorized party may have accessed data in one of its systems. Potentially exposed information includes email addresses, the last four digits and country associated with a credit card, card issuer, expiry date, and payment history, but not full card details. The company said it had no evidence of misuse and warned users to watch for phishing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scribd 通知用户称，其一个系统发生安全事件，未经授权的第三方可能访问了部分数据。可能暴露的信息包括电子邮件地址、信用卡末四位及所属国家、发卡机构、有效期和支付历史，但不包括完整银行卡信息。公司表示没有证据表明这些数据已被滥用，并提醒用户警惕钓鱼攻击。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50270764' data-tags='Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50270764' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dreamagainusa.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wealth Inequality in America drawn to scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国财富不平等被放大呈现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 01:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50270764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50270764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50270764'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A visualization-driven project layers data to show how wealth concentrates at the very top of the US economy, with stark gaps across households. The work underscores how tech platforms and policymakers must consider real distributions when shaping products and policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个以可视化为核心的项目揭示美国经济中财富高度集中的现状，以及家庭之间的巨大差距。此类呈现为科技平台与政策制定者在设计产品与制定政策时提供了对真实分布的直观认知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dreamagainusa.org/img/social-card.png?v=3' data-full='https://dreamagainusa.org/img/social-card.png?v=3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50313661' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50313661' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vitalik.eth.limo/general/2026/10/31/digital_minimalism.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Digital minimalism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数字极简主义</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 23:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50313661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50313661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50313661'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author says they have long favored physical minimalism, owning few possessions and doing more with less. They argue that the idea responded to the conditions of the 2000s and 2010s, while still retaining value today. The author proposes digital minimalism as a new form suited to the modern era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者表示，自己长期推崇物质极简主义，即减少实体物品并以更少资源完成更多事情。作者认为，这一理念回应了 21 世纪初和 2010 年代的环境，但如今仍有价值。作者进一步提出，现代社会需要一种新的极简主义：数字极简主义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://vitalik.ca/images/icon.png' data-full='http://vitalik.ca/images/icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50301854' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50301854' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.binarysecurity.no/posts/2026/10/one-root-case' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Azure&#x27;s Weakest Link – Five Full Cross-Tenant Compromises</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Azure 的薄弱环节——五起跨租户完全妥协</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 03:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50301854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50301854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50301854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A red-team style analysis shows five full cross-tenant compromises in Azure, illustrating how misconfigurations and trust relationships can cascade across an organization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析显示 Azure 中存在五起完全跨租户的妥协，说明错误配置与信任关系如何在企业内级联。文章给出降低风险的实用措施，如严格身份控制、最小权限和加强监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.binarysecurity.no/assets/images/dark_png.png' data-full='https://www.binarysecurity.no/assets/images/dark_png.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50257548' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50257548' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bitlog.com/2026/10/27/software-may-be-getting-worse-but-it-used-to-be-horrible/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software may be getting worse, but it used to be horrible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件可能在变糟，但过去其实更糟</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 04:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 340</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50257548'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that while modern software often feels buggy, earlier eras suffered far worse reliability and UX. Progress came from better tooling, standardization, and automation, but rising complexity now brings new failure modes—so focus on robust testing, observability, and thoughtful UX improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，尽管如今的软件常被感觉不稳定，但过去的软件在可靠性与易用性方面往往更糟。借助更好的工具链、标准化和自动化，软件质量有所提升，但复杂性上升又带来新型故障模式，因此应加强测试、可观测性和用户体验改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bitlog.com/media/fecabf30-3bef-42c9-98d7-337657a2f509/v2/640.jpg' data-full='https://www.bitlog.com/media/fecabf30-3bef-42c9-98d7-337657a2f509/v2/640.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50248061' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50248061' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.panamericandispatch.org/p/meta-employees-denounce-interference' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Employees Denounce Interference in Brazilian Elections</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 员工谴责干涉巴西选举</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 10:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50248061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta workers publicly push back against perceived interference in Brazil&#x27;s electoral process, urging safe, transparent, and privacy-respecting content moderation practices. Their stand signals rising worker activism around tech policy, governance, and the ethics of platform influence in elections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 员工公开反对其平台干预巴西选举的做法，呼吁采用透明、公正且注重隐私的内容治理。此举体现了科技公司治理、伦理与选举干预之间日益显著的员工维权趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!RX_H!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5664bbc8-6981-4cc9-ac02-48d645aa8804_3467x1819.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!RX_H!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5664bbc8-6981-4cc9-ac02-48d645aa8804_3467x1819.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50263854' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50263854' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discord.com/blog/making-content-from-discoverable-servers-easier-to-discover' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discord will soon let anyone find public server messages through Google</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Discord 即将允许通过 Google 搜索公开服务器消息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 11:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50263854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50263854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50263854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discord plans to make content from discoverable servers indexable by Google, improving discoverability but raising moderation and privacy concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Discord 将使公开服务器的内容可被 Google 索引，提升可发现性，但也引发监管与隐私方面的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5f9072399b2640f14d6a2bf4/6ad92479d769414a9fb9c916_bloghero_1800x720.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5f9072399b2640f14d6a2bf4/6ad92479d769414a9fb9c916_bloghero_1800x720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50314666' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50314666' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://piefed.world/post/1503394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Molly (Signal fork) has not been updated since 3 months and is vulnerable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Molly（Signal 分支）已有三个月未更新，存在漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 03:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50314666'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Molly, a Signal fork, had its latest release on July 31, version v8.19.2-4. The supplied information says it has not been updated for roughly three months and is vulnerable to attacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Molly 是 Signal 的一个分支，最近一次发布是在 7 月 31 日，版本为 v8.19.2-4。提供的信息称，它已有约三个月未更新，并且容易受到攻击。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://piefed.world/static/images/piefed_logo_full_t_300x300.svg' data-full='https://piefed.world/static/images/piefed_logo_full_t_300x300.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50265609' data-tags='AI,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50265609' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/early-progress-drug-development' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Early progress on accelerating drug development with Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Claude 加速药物开发的初步进展</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 13:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50265609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50265609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50265609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early results show Claude assisting with drug development tasks such as literature synthesis, hypothesis generation, and experimental design. While promising, the work highlights ongoing questions about reliability, regulatory compliance, and the need for human oversight in AI-augmented drug discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步结果显示 Claude 能协助药物开发任务，如文献综述、假设生成和实验设计。前景虽乐观，但也暴露在可靠性、监管合规性，以及 AI 辅助药物发现需人类监督等方面的持续问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6a50f5ba4c0931f8d2c411f26d860955c1efeae7-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6a50f5ba4c0931f8d2c411f26d860955c1efeae7-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50236081' data-tags='Politics,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50236081' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/europe/serbia-votes-students-seek-unseat-vucic-snap-election-2026-10-25/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Serbia&#x27;s Vucic concedes election defeat in historic win for student movement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>塞尔维亚总统武契奇承认选举失败，学生运动取得历史性胜利</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 14:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50236081'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Serbia&#x27;s president Vučić conceded defeat after a snap election driven by student protests, marking a historic shift in Belgrade politics. The result showcases youth-led mobilization reshaping the country&#x27;s political landscape and could accelerate reforms or policy changes. Analysts will watch for coalition dynamics and governance implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>塞尔维亚总统武契奇在由学生示威推动的提前选举中承认失败，标志着贝尔格莱德政治的历史性转折。此结果显示以青少年为主导的动员正在重塑政治格局，可能推动改革或政策调整。分析师将关注联盟结构及治理走向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50263837' data-tags='Web,Politics,AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50263837' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techpolicy.press/breaking-the-open-web-wont-stop-the-bad-bots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Breaking the open web won&#x27;t stop the bad bots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>破坏开放网络并不能阻止恶意机器人</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 11:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50263837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50263837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50263837'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The authors argue that policymakers should not treat anonymity itself as a problem. They acknowledge that AI developers’ automated crawling is depriving newsrooms of referral traffic and advertising revenue, but say the proposed Stealth Bot Prohibition Act would require automatic identification from anyone accessing a website. In their view, that approach fails to target abusive crawling and threatens a basic tool used to gather public information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为，政策制定者不应把匿名本身视为问题。他们承认，AI 开发者的自动抓取正在让新闻编辑室失去引荐流量和广告收入，但指出拟议的《隐形机器人禁令法》会要求所有自动访问网站的人表明身份及用途。在作者看来，这种做法没有直接针对滥用式抓取，反而会威胁人们获取公开信息的基本工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/3tzzh18d/production/46070e9d00c44c7690c79155723ff59e5c1280fe-1200x675.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/3tzzh18d/production/46070e9d00c44c7690c79155723ff59e5c1280fe-1200x675.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50305573' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50305573' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tangled.org/blog/forge/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The federated code forge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦式代码托管平台</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 08:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50305573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that centralized code forges, which host code and coordinate reviews, are becoming less dependable. It presents Git combined with email as an open alternative that has endured over time, although this approach sacrifices some user experience. The proposed direction is a more federated model for code collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，负责托管代码和协调评审的中心化代码平台正变得不再可靠。它将 Git 与电子邮件结合的方式视为经受时间考验的开放替代方案，尽管这种方式会牺牲部分用户体验。文章提出的方向是更加联邦化的代码协作模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.tangled.network/blog/forge/og.png' data-full='https://assets.tangled.network/blog/forge/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50285452' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50285452' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://smsofdeath.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero click, silent, remote Android DoS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>零点击静默的远程 Android 拒绝服务攻击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 00:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50285452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50285452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50285452'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A zero-click, silent remote DoS vulnerability on Android enables denial-of-service without user interaction, potentially affecting many devices. Exploits could be triggered via crafted traffic or messages, highlighting the urgent need for timely OS patches and mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个无需用户交互、静默发生的 Android 远程拒绝服务漏洞，可能影响大量设备。利用方式可能通过特制网络流量或消息触发，凸显了及时打补丁与缓解措施的紧迫性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50197108' data-tags='Web,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50197108' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://charliegerard.dev/blog/overgrown-extension/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Overgrown, when plants invade your abandoned tabs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Overgrown：当植物侵入你被遗忘的标签页</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 22, 2026 / 08:52 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>疑似中国间谍与黄仁勋关系密切</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 10:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An alleged Chinese intelligence asset is reported to have forged a close relationship with Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang, fueling concerns about insider risk and IP protection in the AI hardware race. The episode underscores how executive networks can become vectors for state-linked influence, pushing firms to tighten access controls and disclosures around partnerships. For technologists, it&#x27;s a reminder to guard sensitive collaborations and monitor for unusual access patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份报道声称一名中国情报人员与 Nvidia 创始人黄仁勋关系密切，引发对内部人风险和知识产权保护的担忧。此事揭示高层网络可能成为国家利益争夺的渠道，促使企业加强访问控制与合作披露。对技术公司而言，需谨慎管理敏感合作与异常访问。</p>

The clip highlights the tension between corporate messaging and civil-liberties concerns around surveillance tech, signaling potential PR and governance risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泄露的音频显示 Flock 的首席执行官对警察声称公众对公司的反对并非真实，暗示公众情绪被夸大或被误解。这一幕凸显了监控技术背后的企业传播与公民自由关注之间的张力，也暴露出公关与治理方面的潜在风险。</p>
