<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50247493' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50247493' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DilawarShafiq/nietzschian-debugger' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nietzschian Debugger</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尼采式调试器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 10:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50247493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50247493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50247493'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows. It’s a thought-provoking take on making debugging less mechanical and more narrative.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。它挑战传统的机械式排错，试图让调试过程更具叙事性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/152be97e099452e96da44b634e34160794ba5b4a5475b0763acfff28d1ffdea2/DilawarShafiq/nietzschian-debugger' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/152be97e099452e96da44b634e34160794ba5b4a5475b0763acfff28d1ffdea2/DilawarShafiq/nietzschian-debugger' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50314085' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50314085' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xoranth.net/gcc-switch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switch Lowering in GCC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GCC 中的 switch 降低</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 01:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50314085'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It asks whether the compiler’s decision-tree implementation is the only option and focuses on GCC’s lowering strategies. Except for bitset lowering, the optimizations discussed also translate to Clang.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章研究 GCC 如何实现 switch 语句。它探讨编译器采用决策树实现是否是唯一选择，并重点分析 GCC 的多种降低策略。除位集降低外，文中讨论的优化也能对应到 Clang。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50235217' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50235217' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/10/personalized-mrna-cancer-vaccine-cut-melanoma-death-recurrence-by-42/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moderna and Merck say their mRNA cancer vaccine cut death, recurrence by 42%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moderna 与 Merck 表示其个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗将死亡率与复发率降低约 42%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 12:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50235217'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moderna and Merck report that their personalized mRNA cancer vaccine reduced death and recurrence by about 42% in a melanoma study. The finding underscores the potential of mRNA cancer vaccines to change oncology outcomes, though results in larger trials and real-world use remain to be seen. If confirmed, it could accelerate investment in personalized vaccines and biomarker-driven therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moderna 与 Merck 报告，其个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗在黑色素瘤研究中将死亡率和复发率降低约 42%。这一发现凸显 mRNA 疫苗在肿瘤治疗中的潜力，但需要在更大规模试验和实际应用中证实。若得到证实，可能推动个性化疫苗和基因标志物驱动疗法的投资与开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-2260256951-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-2260256951-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50275373' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50275373' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reddit.com/r/Seattle/comments/1xhf6qp/archivetoday_links_going_forward/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Archive.today links going forward</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Archive.today 链接将进入新阶段</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 08:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50275373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50275373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50275373'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archive.today announced changes to how its links work going forward, signaling shifts in archiving behavior and access. The update may impact researchers relying on stable snapshots and could influence how citations reference preserved content in the future.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Archive.today 宣布将对链接的工作方式进行调整，标志着存档行为的变化。此举可能影响依赖稳健快照的研究人员，以及在引用受保内容时的可追溯性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50309266' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50309266' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencenorway.no/archaeology-climate-culture/the-oldest-ice-is-disappearing-the-stone-age-is-emerging-from-the-melting-ice/2713921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The oldest ice disappears and the Stone Age emerges from it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最古老的冰层正在消失，石器时代由此重现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 12:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50309266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50309266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50309266'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists in Innlandet, eastern Norway, have spent 20 years recovering artifacts emerging from melting mountain ice. Earlier finds were mostly no more than 2,000 years old and often dated to the Viking Age, but 6,000-year-old arrowheads are now appearing. The researchers describe the melting ice as rapidly changing the archaeological landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>挪威东部因兰内特的考古学家花了 20 年时间，持续发掘和保护从山地融冰中出现的文物。过去发现的物品大多不超过 2,000 年历史，许多来自维京时代；如今，已有 6,000 年历史的箭头出土。研究人员表示，冰层融化正在迅速改变考古景观。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.sciencenorway.no/2713469.jpg?imageId=2713469&amp;x=0&amp;y=17.9&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=73.3&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=17.9&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=73.3&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.sciencenorway.no/2713469.jpg?imageId=2713469&amp;x=0&amp;y=17.9&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=73.3&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=17.9&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=73.3&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50320588' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50320588' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001625-understanding-chatgpt-and-codex-usage-limits' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT and Codex usage limits merging</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 与 Codex 的使用额度将合并</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 14:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50320588'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starting in December, Plus, Pro, and Business plans will share one usage allowance across ChatGPT and Codex as agentic Work capabilities move into ChatGPT. Usage will be measured in tokens and will vary with the selected model and task. Instant chat will not consume the allowance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从 12 月起，Plus、Pro 和 Business 计划将在 ChatGPT 与 Codex 之间共享同一个使用额度，随着 Work 的代理能力并入 ChatGPT。使用量将按 token 计算，并取决于所选模型和任务。即时聊天不会消耗该额度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://help.openai.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://help.openai.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50290652' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50290652' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thedataexchange.media/leo-de-moura-lean-aws/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI, Lean, and the Future of Formal Verification, with Lean Creator Leo de Moura</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI、Lean 与形式验证的未来：对话 Lean 创造者 Leo de Moura</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 08:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50290652'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leo de Moura, creator of Lean and an AWS distinguished scientist, discusses how AI could make formal verification more accessible. The conversation covers specifications, machine-checked proofs, trust in AI-generated code and autonomous agents, and the prospect of a verified software stack. It also considers how these developments could affect mathematics and the careers of engineers and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lean 创造者、AWS 杰出科学家 Leo de Moura 讨论了 AI 如何让形式验证更易于使用。对话涉及规范、机器检查证明、如何信任 AI 生成的代码和自主代理，以及构建经过验证的软件栈的可能性。内容还讨论了这些发展对数学以及工程师和研究人员职业的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/thedataexchange.media/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Leo-de-Moura-art.001.jpeg?fit=1920%2C1080&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/thedataexchange.media/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Leo-de-Moura-art.001.jpeg?fit=1920%2C1080&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50251127' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50251127' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://corridorkey.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CorridorKey 2.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CorridorKey 2.0</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 14:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50251127'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CorridorKey 2.0 introduces a new iteration of its privacy-preserving authentication or data-sharing protocol, improving security guarantees and developer ergonomics. It enables streamlined, low-friction, passwordless sign-ins or secure data flows across services while reducing data exposure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CorridorKey 2.0 推出隐私保护认证/数据共享协议的新一代，提升安全性和开发体验。它实现更无缝的无密码认证或跨服务的安全数据流，同时降低数据暴露风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://corridorkey.com/og-card.png' data-full='https://corridorkey.com/og-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50315226' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50315226' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nullprogram.com/blog/2026/10/31/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prospero: A new coding harness for a more civilized age</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Prospero：为更文明的时代打造的新型编码工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 04:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50315226'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prospero is a coding harness built for local and open-weight LLMs. Its author found existing harnesses unnecessarily heavy because many use web stacks, so they built a C++ application with a Dear ImGui docking interface that uses roughly two orders of magnitude fewer resources without sacrificing features. Prospero includes no model providers; users must supply their own through options such as llama.cpp, OpenRouter, Anthropic, or OpenAI APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Prospero 是一个为本地和开放权重大语言模型打造的编码工具。作者认为现有工具大多使用网页技术栈、资源消耗过高，因此用 C++ 和 Dear ImGui 停靠式界面制作了一个功能不减、资源消耗约低两个数量级的应用。Prospero 不内置模型提供商，用户需要通过 llama.cpp、OpenRouter、Anthropic 或 OpenAI API 等方式自行提供模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nullprogram.com/img/prospero/screenshot.png' data-full='https://nullprogram.com/img/prospero/screenshot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50272596' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50272596' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/video/2026/10/28/spain-first-footage-reveals-wreckage-of-deadly-high-speed-train-crash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two high-speed trains collide near Toledo, Spain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙托莱多附近两列高铁相撞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 05:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50272596'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two high-speed trains collided near Toledo, Spain, with casualties and a wrecked scene captured in initial footage. Investigations will focus on signaling, safety protocols, and emergency response, with potential implications for Europe’s rail safety standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙托莱多附近两列高速列车相撞，造成人员伤亡，现场残骸初步画面已公开。调查将聚焦信号系统、安全规程与应急响应，或对欧洲铁路安全标准产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/97/01/65/1200x675_cmsv2_460f38d5-46e5-5063-b7df-c35e0d4f645f-9970165.jpg' data-full='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/97/01/65/1200x675_cmsv2_460f38d5-46e5-5063-b7df-c35e0d4f645f-9970165.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50243762' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50243762' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freedom.press/the-classifieds/doj-says-presidents-should-be-able-to-sell-secrets-on-ebay/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOJ says presidents should be able to sell secrets on eBay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DOJ 表示总统应能在 eBay 出售秘密</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 06:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50243762'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece imagines a world where information control is so lax that presidents could sell secrets on eBay, using the scenario to critique the absurdity and danger of current classification practices. It argues for stronger transparency and reform of the rules governing classified information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇观点文章以极端设想总统能在 eBay 出售秘密来讽刺现行保密制度的荒诞与风险，提出应加强透明度并改革机密信息的管理规则。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.freedom.press/media/images/NSA_Cryptolog_Nov1978.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x630.png' data-full='https://media.freedom.press/media/images/NSA_Cryptolog_Nov1978.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x630.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50319110' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50319110' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fullycharged.show/podcasts/podcast-361-why-electrification-could-cut-global-energy-use-in-half/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electrification Could Cut Global Energy Use in Half</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电气化可能让全球能源使用量减半</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 12:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50319110'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Professor Jan Rosenow says electricity currently represents only about 20% of the global energy picture, with transport and heating still dominated by fossil fuels. The current system wastes roughly two-thirds of its energy inputs as heat. The discussion argues that large-scale electrification could halve total global energy demand.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jan Rosenow 教授表示，电力目前只占全球能源格局的约 20%，交通和供暖仍主要依赖化石燃料。现有系统会将约三分之二的能源输入以热量形式浪费掉。讨论认为，大规模电气化可能使全球总能源需求减半。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fullycharged.show/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Banner-why-electrification-could-cut-global-energy-use-in-half-pc361.jpg' data-full='https://fullycharged.show/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Banner-why-electrification-could-cut-global-energy-use-in-half-pc361.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50287599' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50287599' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mastodon.sdf.org/@netbsd/117518160563539748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NetBSD adds a new text editor called atto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NetBSD 推出面向初学者的文本编辑器 Atto</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 04:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50287599'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD now includes Atto, a beginner-friendly text editor built into the system, aiming to lower the barrier to editing on BSD. It provides a simpler alternative to traditional editors like Vim or Nano, helping new users and admins configure systems more easily. This could encourage wider BSD adoption among newcomers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 基础系统新增 Atto，一款面向初学者的文本编辑器，降低在 BSD 上进行文本编辑的门槛。它提供比 Vim 或 Nano 更简化的编辑体验，帮助新手与管理员更轻松地修改配置、管理系统。这一举措或将提升新用户对 BSD 的接受度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mastodon.sdf.org/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mastodon.sdf.org/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>怪物之地：代理人完成工作后你还需要知道什么</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 06:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50258868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50258868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50258868'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that relying on AI agents doesn&#x27;t eliminate the need for human oversight. It discusses what questions remain about reliability, accountability, and the limits of delegated work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文認為即使将任务交给 AI 代理也不能完全放手，仍需人类监督。讨论了在可靠性、问责与委托工作界限方面尚需解决的问题。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50239214' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://divemagazine.com/scuba-diving-news/ss-united-states-to-be-sunk-as-worlds-largest-artificial-reef-in-november' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SS United States to be sunk as largest artificial reef</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SS United States 将被沉没，成为全球最大的人工礁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 21:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50239214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50239214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50239214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plans to sink the SS United States to become the world’s largest artificial reef aim to create a new underwater habitat and attract divers. The project also seeks to preserve parts of the ship’s legacy for historical and educational purposes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将SS United States沉入海底，打造全球最大的人工礁，旨在为海洋生物提供新栖息地并促进潜水旅游。该项目还计划保留船体的历史遗产以供教育与纪念。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://divemagazine.com/wp-content/uploads/ss-united-states-at-anchor-2017.webp' data-full='https://divemagazine.com/wp-content/uploads/ss-united-states-at-anchor-2017.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 18:03 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237712'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study showing that dropping Next.js boosted Lighthouse scores to 100 across categories, illustrating how framework choices impact performance audits. It emphasizes practical steps for lean sites.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过移除 Next.js，博客在 Lighthouse 的所有分类指标上都达到100的案例，展示了框架选择对性能审计的直接影响。给出让网站更高效的实际做法。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PHP2Shell 漏洞利用持续针对 PHP 服务器获取 shell 的攻击，攻击载荷与规避手段呈现改进趋势。缓解措施包括打补丁、禁用高危函数、监控异常行为等。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50237224' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Attention Is Not What You Need (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>注意力不是你需要的（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 16:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237224'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2025 paper argues that attention is not the ultimate solution for scalable AI, proposing alternative architectures and training paradigms. It challenges the dominance of attention-based models for long-range dependencies and outlines practical directions for future work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇2025年的论文提出，注意力机制并非解决所有问题的万能钥匙，提出了替代的结构与训练范式。它质疑了注意力在处理长距离依赖中的主导地位，并勾勒出未来工作的现实方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50220975' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50220975' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lede-v2.nautil.us/why-birds-can-fly-over-mount-everest-237864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why birds can fly over Mount Everest (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么鸟类能飞越珠穆朗玛峰</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 06:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50220975'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Birds can fly over Mount Everest thanks to specialized physiology and high-altitude flight strategies that mitigate hypoxia. The article explains how certain species endure extreme elevations and what limits them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鸟类能飞越珠穆朗玛峰，靠的是对高海拔缺氧有针对性的生理适应与飞行策略。文章解释了哪些物种能在极端海拔下生存以及它们的极限在哪里。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lede-admin.nautil.us/wp-content/uploads/sites/70/17336_e900ad3b2019268e3182123407fda545.png' data-full='https://lede-admin.nautil.us/wp-content/uploads/sites/70/17336_e900ad3b2019268e3182123407fda545.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50260753' data-tags='Show HN,Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50260753' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zebra.rodeo/en/blog/marelleo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Marelleo, an open-source story box I built for my kids using ESP32</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Marelleo——我用 ESP32 为孩子制作的开源故事盒</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 08:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50260753'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Marelleo is a box that starts playing a story when a child places a figurine on top and stops when the figurine is removed. The creator built it for three children despite having no prior electronics experience, learning through breadboards, failed components, multiple enclosures, and a custom printed board. The design was inspired by the simple interaction of the Toniebox.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Marelleo 是一个故事盒：孩子把 figurine 放在盒子顶部就会开始播放故事，拿走后便停止。创作者此前不懂电子技术，却为自己的三个孩子制作了它，并在面包板、烧坏的电位器、多个外壳和定制电路板的反复试错中完成学习。这一设计受到 Toniebox 简单交互方式的启发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zebra.rodeo/assets/images/marelleo/og.jpg' data-full='https://zebra.rodeo/assets/images/marelleo/og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50209278' data-tags='Science'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50252159' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/kv-cache-exposure-remediation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cross-request KV cache exposure in the GLM-5.3 Workers AI model (155 responses)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.3 Workers AI 模型的跨请求 KV 缓存暴露及应对</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 15:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50252159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50252159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50252159'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare discloses a cross-request KV cache exposure in the GLM-5.3 Workers model, exposing potential data leakage across requests. The company released remediation guidance and urged developers to tighten cache scopes and access controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 公布 GLM-5.3 Workers AI 模型存在跨请求 KV 缓存暴露，可能导致请求间数据泄露。公司提供修复指南，敦促开发者收紧缓存作用域与访问控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M5X2YJT16K16BFFJGEMCAXRS.01M5X2YM62P3YQRBGRP96FS4P6.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M5X2YJT16K16BFFJGEMCAXRS.01M5X2YM62P3YQRBGRP96FS4P6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inventor7777.github.io/blog/2026/10/22/debug-access-on-brother/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Getting debug access on a Brother printer over Telnet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 Telnet 获取 Brother 打印机的调试访问权限</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 06:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50243757'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demonstration of gaining debug access on a Brother printer via Telnet exposes insecure debugging interfaces and potential control over the device. The post advises disabling Telnet, updating firmware, and hardening network security to mitigate risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示通过 Telnet 获取 Brother 打印机的调试访问，暴露了调试接口的安全隐患。建议禁用 Telnet、更新固件并加强网络安全来降低风险。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://doublepulsar.com/anthropic-hacked-to-deliver-remote-access-trojan-to-claude-developer-laptops-worldwide-1148a92baae2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic hacked to deliver remote access trojan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 遭黑客入侵，向 Claude 开发者笔记本投放远程访问木马</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 03:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50301983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50301983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50301983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A recent security post alleges Anthropic was hacked to deliver a remote access Trojan to Claude developers’ laptops globally. The incident underscores how threat actors can weaponize AI-related tooling supply chains and developer environments, prompting improved phishing defenses and endpoint protection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新安全报告称 Anthropic 遭黑客入侵，借 Remote Access Trojan 针对全球 Claude 开发者笔记本。事件凸显攻击者如何利用 AI 相关工具链与开发环境进行渗透，促使加强钓鱼防护与端点安全。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50255343' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cuis.st/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cuis: The Modern Smalltalk-80</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cuis：现代 Smalltalk-80 实现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 23:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50255343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50255343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50255343'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cuis is a modern reimplementation of Smalltalk-80, bringing the classic language to contemporary platforms with a lightweight VM and integrated tooling. It underscores a revival approach that preserves live-object programming and image-based workflows while improving accessibility and packaging for today’s developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cuis 是对 Smalltalk-80 的现代化再实现，带来跨平台的轻量虚拟机与整合开发工具。它延续了现场对象编程与图像化工作流的传统，同时提升了可访问性与现代化打包。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318871' data-tags='AI,Gaming,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318871' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryanmadden.net/gpt-56-beats-factorio/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 Sol can beat Factorio on peaceful mode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol 能在和平模式下击败《异星工厂》</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 11:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318871'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol, running in Codex with low thinking level, completed a peaceful Factorio run in 13.5 hours of game time. The run used a Python-based game manager that let the model interact programmatically while enforcing restrictions such as player reach and fog of war. A timelapse of the complete run is also available. The result extends earlier experiments using AI agents to play Factorio autonomously.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>运行在低思考级别 Codex 中的 GPT-5.6 Sol，用13.5小时游戏时间完成了《异星工厂》的和平模式流程。实验使用基于 Python 的游戏管理器，让模型以程序方式操作游戏，同时遵守玩家操作范围和战争迷雾等限制。完整过程还制作成了延时视频。这一结果延续了此前让 AI 代理自主游玩《异星工厂》的实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ryanmadden.net/img/factorio_rocket_launch_social.jpg' data-full='https://ryanmadden.net/img/factorio_rocket_launch_social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50320255' data-tags='AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50320255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mashable.com/tech/anthropic-takes-steps-to-block-video-game-cloning-and-reverse-engineering' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic takes steps to block Claude from reverse-engineering video games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 采取措施阻止 Claude 逆向工程电子游戏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 14:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50320255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude has reportedly begun restricting work involving video-game modding, decompilation, and reverse engineering. The restrictions are broad enough to affect legitimate developers and basic troubleshooting. No official explanation has been announced, although the changes come amid growing concern about AI-assisted game hacking and modding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Claude 开始限制涉及电子游戏改 mod、反编译和逆向工程的工作。这些限制范围很广，已经影响到合法开发者和基本故障排查。官方尚未公布解释，但这些变化发生在外界日益关注 AI 辅助游戏破解和改 mod 之际。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/00cSAXoWdRYObLNtN71MkCO/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1793455880.png' data-full='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/00cSAXoWdRYObLNtN71MkCO/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1793455880.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50245014' data-tags='Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50245014' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hothardware.com/news/why-micron-is-suddenly-ditching-4f-dram' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Micron Is Suddenly Ditching 4F² DRAM as Rivals Double Down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美光突然放弃 4F² DRAM，竞争对手加码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 08:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50245014'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Micron is reportedly ditching its 4F² DRAM process as rivals push to scale advanced DRAM nodes. The move could affect supply dynamics and pricing as vendors fight for yield and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美光据称放弃 4F² DRAM 制程，竞争对手正加码推进更先进的 DRAM 芯片。此举可能改变供给格局和定价，因厂商之间在良率与性能方面展开角力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.hothardware.com/contentimages/newsitem/71935/content/16x9_2133x1200_highres-micron-boise-fab-photo.jpg' data-full='https://images.hothardware.com/contentimages/newsitem/71935/content/16x9_2133x1200_highres-micron-boise-fab-photo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50290283' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50290283' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trakkr.ai/trakkr-research/zombie-stats' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zombie stats: Where the numbers AI quotes come from</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>僵尸数据：AI 引用的数字来自哪里</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 08:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50290283'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into the provenance of statistics AI quotes, exposing how numbers can be misattributed, outdated, or misleading. The piece argues for rigorous data provenance and better tooling to audit AI-generated claims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入分析 AI 所引用统计数据的来源，揭示数字可能被错引、过时或误导。呼吁加强数据溯源和工具，提升 AI 生成信息的可信度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://trakkr.ai/images/research/og/zombie-stats.png' data-full='https://trakkr.ai/images/research/og/zombie-stats.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50298964' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/picocss/pico/discussions/754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pico CSS is no longer maintained</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pico CSS 不再维护</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 18:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50298964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50298964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50298964'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility. The post may offer guidance on how to migrate or handle breaking changes while preserving the lightweight, accessible styling Pico CSS is known for.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。文中给出迁移建议与应对潜在改动的途径，同时保留 Pico CSS 的简洁与可访问性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ubicloud.com/blog/fixing-tool-call-accuracy-in-vllm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fixing Tool-Call Accuracy in vLLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>修正 vLLM 中的工具调用准确性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 05:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50242870'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post outlines improvements to tool-calling accuracy in vLLM-based systems, addressing misrouting and erroneous tool usage. These changes enhance reliability when integrating external tools into LLM workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中介绍了在基于 vLLM 的系统中改进工具调用准确性的工作，解决了错误路由与错误的工具使用问题，提高了在LLM工作流中整合外部工具的可靠性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50233059' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/e-n-f/if-then-else/blob/master/if-then-else.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If-then-else had to be invented (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>if-then-else 需要被发明吗（2020）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 09:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50233059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A speculative piece arguing about why conditional constructs like if-then-else are essential in programming language design, exploring historical aspects and potential alternatives. It challenges assumptions about how we structure logic in code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇关于在编程语言设计中 if-then-else 条件表达式为何重要的讨论性文章，回顾历史背景并探讨潜在替代方案，质疑我们如何在代码中组织逻辑。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50271685' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50271685' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.apple.com/en-us/149555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>About the compromised eMudhra intermediate CAs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 eMudhra 中级证书被妥协</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 04:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50271685'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An advisory notes that eMudhra&#x27;s intermediate Certificate Authorities were compromised, prompting revocation and reissuance guidance. The incident highlights PKI trust risks and the need for organizations to monitor certificates and update trust stores across devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公告称 eMudhra 的中级证书遭到妥协，建议撤销并重新颁发。此事件凸显公钥基础设施的信任风险，促使组织加强证书生命周期管理与设备信任库更新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://support.apple.com/beacon.png' data-full='https://support.apple.com/beacon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318189' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318189' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/politics/2026/oct/05/green-party-nuclear-power-policy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK Green party drops longstanding opposition to nuclear power</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国绿党放弃长期反对核能的立场</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318189'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Green Party members in England and Wales voted to end the party’s opposition to nuclear power. A conference motion removed its blanket opposition to nuclear fission and committed the party to assessing nuclear projects alongside other low-carbon technologies. The decision represents a major shift in its energy policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英格兰和威尔士的绿党成员投票决定结束该党反对核能的立场。党代会通过的动议取消了对核裂变的全面反对，并承诺将核项目与其他低碳技术一并评估。这一决定标志着其能源政策发生重大转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/dc74a28ef57e469544afa9725c980e5365548295/1072_491_2108_1687/master/2108.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=513fa6e2a73e87072f6ec98718a9ba24' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/dc74a28ef57e469544afa9725c980e5365548295/1072_491_2108_1687/master/2108.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=513fa6e2a73e87072f6ec98718a9ba24' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50292233' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50292233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vut.cz/en/but/news-f19528/two-but-graduates-received-state-honors-from-the-president-of-the-republic-d378468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tomáš Mikolov awarded the Medal of Merit by the President of the Czech Republic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>捷克总统授予托马斯·米科洛夫勋章</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 09:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50292233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50292233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50292233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Czech Republic honors Tomáš Mikolov for his foundational contributions to word embeddings and neural language processing. The award underscores the impact of his work on modern NLP and the broader European AI ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>捷克共和国为词嵌入与神经语言处理领域的奠基性贡献者托马什·米科洛夫颁发勋章，体现其对现代 NLP 和欧洲 AI 生态的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vut.cz/i/media/document_images/fotogalerie_doc/ostra/378468/65d4_1600.jpeg' data-full='https://www.vut.cz/i/media/document_images/fotogalerie_doc/ostra/378468/65d4_1600.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50322022' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50322022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaking Secrets from Air-Gap Computers by Spelling Radio Signals from RAM (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过从 RAM 拼写无线电信号，从气隙计算机窃取秘密（2024）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 18:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50322022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50322022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50322022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe an attack in which malware on an air-gapped computer generates radio signals through memory buses. The signals can encode files, images, keystrokes, biometric data, and encryption keys, which an attacker can intercept using software-defined radio and an inexpensive antenna. The paper presents the attack&#x27;s design, implementation, and evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员描述了一种攻击：气隙计算机上的恶意软件通过内存总线生成无线电信号。这些信号可以编码文件、图像、键盘记录、生物特征数据和加密密钥，攻击者则可使用软件定义无线电和廉价天线进行拦截。论文介绍了该攻击的设计、实现和评估结果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50237379' data-tags='Security,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50237379' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/KillaBoi/StealMyBitcoin-PoC' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steal My Bitcoin CTF PoC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steal My Bitcoin CTF PoC</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 17:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237379'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A PoC for a Bitcoin-themed Capture The Flag exercise demonstrates a vulnerability or attack vector in simulated wallets. It underscores how responsible disclosure and educational CTFs can teach secure crypto practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个比特币主题CTF的漏洞证明，演示了在模拟钱包环境中的攻击向量。强调通过负责任披露和教育性CTF提升加密安全意识。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a4bd5ef5c90019e021aee5716cab458c7a95dbb6fe1f8eb02543e5d175f009a2/KillaBoi/StealMyBitcoin-PoC' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a4bd5ef5c90019e021aee5716cab458c7a95dbb6fe1f8eb02543e5d175f009a2/KillaBoi/StealMyBitcoin-PoC' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50280178' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50280178' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/10/google-ai-gets-cagey-when-asked-who-won-the-2020-elections/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google AI gets cagey when asked who won the 2020 elections</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50310398' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50310398' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.firezone.dev/blog/introducing-sanstun' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sanstun: A sans-IO, parallel-friendly WireGuard implementation in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sanstun：用 Rust 实现的无 IO、适合并行的 WireGuard</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 14:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50310398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50310398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50310398'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sanstun is a sans-IO implementation of the WireGuard state machine written in Rust. It is designed around ease of use, security, performance, and a parallel-friendly API. The sans-IO approach separates input/output and side effects, including time and randomness, from state and business logic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sanstun 是一个用 Rust 编写的 WireGuard 状态机 sans-IO 实现。它以易用性、安全性、性能和适合并行的 API 为设计目标。sans-IO 方法将输入输出及时间、随机数等副作用，与状态和业务逻辑分离。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.firezone.dev/blog/opengraph-image?dc0a4d310bb2b9d5' data-full='https://www.firezone.dev/blog/opengraph-image?dc0a4d310bb2b9d5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50209525' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50209525' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.df.com/diamond-transistor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Diamond Transistor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>钻石晶体管</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 07:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50209525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50209525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50209525'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diamond-based transistors promise superior thermal conductivity and wide bandgap, enabling higher power density and faster switching. Real-world adoption faces fabrication challenges and cost, but the approach could influence high-frequency and power electronics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于钻石的晶体管承诺提供优越的热导性和宽带隙，带来更高的功率密度和更快的开关速度。实际应用仍受制于制造工艺与成本，但其潜力会影响高频与電力电子领域。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6a96e5c74cce21a226992e29/6ac00c59fff6a4c32add55dd_OpenGraph.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6a96e5c74cce21a226992e29/6ac00c59fff6a4c32add55dd_OpenGraph.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。它展示了在消费级硬件上实现私有、离线推理的可行性，但模型规模与计算需求仍是实际制约。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国和加拿大的已失传萤火虫物种研究</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50224079'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research catalogs firefly species in the US and Canada that are extinct or likely extinct, discussing drivers like habitat loss and climate pressures. <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50309314' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50309314' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blue-continuum.com/verne-paris' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jules Verne&#x27;s Lost Novel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>儒勒·凡尔纳遗失的小说</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50309314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50309314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50309314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In 1863, Jules Verne imagined Paris in 1960 in a novel later titled “Paris in the Twentieth Century.” His publisher rejected it as implausible and too bleak, and the manuscript remained hidden for 131 years before being recovered and published in France in 1994. The novel envisioned a civilization shaped by science, machinery, commerce, and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1863 年，儒勒·凡尔纳在后来被称为《二十世纪的巴黎》的小说中设想了 1960 年的巴黎。出版商认为作品不可信且过于悲观而拒绝出版，手稿隐藏了 131 年，直到被发现并于 1994 年在法国出版。小说描绘了一个由科学、机器、商业和效率塑造的文明。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blue-continuum.com/__l5e/assets-v1/4c93ed3c-ba50-4d5d-a01a-8490d86ffb63/verne-paris-hero.jpeg' data-full='https://blue-continuum.com/__l5e/assets-v1/4c93ed3c-ba50-4d5d-a01a-8490d86ffb63/verne-paris-hero.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50280722' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50280722' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://v12.sh/blog/the-case-of-the-9bit-aes-key' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case of the 9 bit AES key</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>九位AES密钥之谜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 13:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50280722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50280722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50280722'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog post explores what happens if an AES key were only 9 bits long, showing how brutally small key space collapses brute-force resistance. It emphasizes why modern cryptography uses 128-bit, 192-bit, or 256-bit keys and how implementation constraints can inadvertently weaken security. The piece also serves as a cautionary tale about key management and API design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇博文探讨如果 AES 密钥只有 9 位会怎样，展示了极小的密钥空间将如何迅速削弱暴力破解的对抗性。文章强调现代密码学为何采用 128 位及更高位数的密钥，以及实现层面的约束如何无意中削弱安全性。该文也提醒在密钥管理与 API 设计中要避免此类风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://v12.sh/static/opengraph-image.png' data-full='https://v12.sh/static/opengraph-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50290912' data-tags='AI,DevOps,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50290912' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://skypilot.ai/blog/grace-blackwell-needs-a-new-scheduler' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grace Blackwell needs a new scheduler: 27% more usable GPUs from the same racks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grace Blackwell 需要一个新的调度器：在相同机架上挖掘 27% 的 GPU 可用性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 08:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50290912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50290912'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A scheduler redesign dramatically improves GPU utilization for multi-tenant AI workloads, squeezing 27% more usable GPUs from the same rack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调度器的改进带来多租户 AI 工作负载下显著的 GPU 利用率提升，在不增硬件的前提下从同一机架挤出约 27% 的可用 GPU。文章分析了打包、隔离和调度策略等设计要点，以及对系统 throughput 的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/9qcljuzt/production/3cb7da5832bff00b2dd5c3fd21fc742b3e0a09ae-3840x2160.png?rect=0,77,3840,2006&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/9qcljuzt/production/3cb7da5832bff00b2dd5c3fd21fc742b3e0a09ae-3840x2160.png?rect=0,77,3840,2006&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50251070' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20261026-00/?p=112797/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why a program doesn&#x27;t work when run from a batch file, but works when clicked</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么程序从批处理文件运行时不起作用，但直接点击就能运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 14:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50251070'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains why a Windows program can fail when launched from a batch file but succeed when run from its icon, tracing issues to working directory, relative paths, and environment variables that differ between contexts. It offers practical debugging tips to reproduce and fix the discrepancy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中解释了为何从批处理文件启动程序可能失败、直接点击运行却能成功，原因在于工作目录、相对路径与环境变量在不同上下文中的差异。并给出可操作的调试要点，帮助重现并修复此类问题。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237078'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A postmortem on porting F-Zero X to the Nintendo 3DS, detailing technical hurdles, optimizations, and the trade-offs of a handheld port. It offers lessons for emulation, optimization, and cross-platform development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>F-Zero X 移植到任天堂3DS 的事后总结，揭示了技术难题、优化策略与 handheld 端口的权衡，为反向工程、优化以及跨平台开发提供经验。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50280662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50280662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50280662'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A leak site published tens of thousands of DHS employees&#x27; data, raising privacy and security concerns. The release could expose undercover workers and disrupt agency operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个泄漏网站公开数万名国土安全部员工的资料，带来隐私与安全方面的担忧。该信息可能暴露 undercover 工作者并影响机构的数据治理与应急响应。</p>
