<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50253939' data-tags='Programming,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50253939' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eliasfarhan.ch/graphics/cpp/2026/01/27/why-i-teach-opengles.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I still teach OpenGL ES 3.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我仍在教授 OpenGL ES 3.0</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 19:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50253939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenGL ES 3.0 remains a solid teaching foundation even as newer APIs emerge. The author argues for teaching 3.0 to instill core concepts like the programmable pipeline, shader stages, and cross-platform practice, before tackling newer APIs. This approach helps students build a durable mental model of graphics rendering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为即便有更新的 API，OpenGL ES 3.0 仍是学习图形编程的扎实基础。文中讨论了从3.0着手的教学要点、如何传授可移植性以及为何要在入门阶段打好管线和着色器等核心概念。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50291421' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50291421' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://16colo.rs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>16colo.rs – ANSI/ASCII art archive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>16colo.rs：ANSI/ASCII 艺术档案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 09:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50291421'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>16colo.rs preserves a vibrant subculture of ANSI/ASCII art, chronicling scenes from BBS and early internet days. The archive is a treasure trove for retro computing fans and a reminder of how terse text grew into a visual language.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>16colo.rs 收藏并展示了 ANSI/ASCII 艺术的丰富子文化，追忆 BBS 时代与早期互联网场景。这个档案是复古计算爱好者的宝库，也提醒人们文本如何演化为一种视觉语言。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://16colo.rs/static/16c-og.png' data-full='https://16colo.rs/static/16c-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50253529' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50253529' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/otya128/winevdm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>16-bit Windows (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0, 3.1, etc.) on 64-bit Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在64位 Windows 上运行16位 Windows 应用（1.x/2.x/3.0/3.1 等）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 18:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50253529'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime. It illustrates how modern Windows still lacks native 16-bit support, leaving preservation-focused projects to recreate the old environment. This matters for running legacy software, archaeology of software, and niche users who still rely on early Windows programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。与现代 Windows 不再原生支持16位应用不同，兼容层/运行时的实现让老旧软件得以保存与使用，但仍存在兼容性与性能限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0b0b8e179b49c4b2f40eb8f39317e8fe1c2a7d260496387139be363075e9c83c/otya128/winevdm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0b0b8e179b49c4b2f40eb8f39317e8fe1c2a7d260496387139be363075e9c83c/otya128/winevdm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50297089' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50297089' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://journal.animationstudies.org/article/id/66/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Pig, the State, and War: Porco Rosso (Kurenai No Buta) (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>猪、国家与战争：2014年的《红猪》研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 15:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50297089'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A scholarly reading of Porco Rosso examines how a pig pilot doubles as a critique of war, fascism, and propaganda within a whimsical, airborne world. The piece places the film in Studio Ghibli’s anti-war tradition and uses its satirical tone to illuminate questions about heroism and nationalism. It offers fresh framing for understanding the film’s political subtext.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对《红猪》的学术解读探讨了猪头驾驶员如何成为对战争、法西斯主义与宣传的批判载体，在一个充满想象的空中世界中呈现。论文将影片置于吉卜力工作室的反战传统之中，利用讽刺语气揭示英雄主义与民族主义间的张力。为理解影片的政治潜台词提供了新的视角。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://journal.animationstudies.org/media/cover_images/32a1852c-fcda-4630-b063-f82dd00354b3.jpeg' data-full='https://journal.animationstudies.org/media/cover_images/32a1852c-fcda-4630-b063-f82dd00354b3.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50275128' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50275128' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/blog/nvidia/nemotron-transcribe-granary' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe: open ASR for 40 languages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe：40 种语言的开源 ASR</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 08:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50275128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50275128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50275128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications. The release lets researchers compare performance against other open models and accelerates progress in open speech AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。该发布使研究者能够与其它开源模型进行对比，加速开放语音 AI 的进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-uploads.huggingface.co/production/uploads/68127936de624fb6f57d9989/3vFxCEUXz_xJVrTBr4hNr.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn-uploads.huggingface.co/production/uploads/68127936de624fb6f57d9989/3vFxCEUXz_xJVrTBr4hNr.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50200957' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50200957' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archaeologymag.com/2026/09/iron-age-salt-mining-complex-beneath-hallstatt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iron Age salt-mining complex found beneath Hallstatt in Austria</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>奥地利哈尔施塔特地下发现铁器时代盐矿遗址</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 22, 2026 / 14:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50200957'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists uncover an Iron Age salt-mining complex beneath Hallstatt, revealing sophisticated mining operations and long-distance trade networks. The discovery enriches understanding of alpine economies and ancient technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家在奥地利哈尔施塔特地下发现铁器时代的盐矿遗址，展示了复杂的矿业作业和远距离贸易网络。这一发现丰富了对阿尔卑斯山地区古代经济与技术的理解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50266719' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50266719' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://felixrieseberg.com/hunger-beats-tenure-advice-for-junior-engineers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hunger beats tenure: Advice for junior engineers</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 15:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50266719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50266719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hunger beats tenure offers practical guidance for junior engineers to prioritize impact and continuous learning over staying safe in a comfortable job. It advocates grabbing ownership, shipping early, seeking feedback, and building a strong track record to accelerate growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章为初级工程师提供实用建议，强调把影响力和持续学习放在优先位置，而非追求安稳的任期。倡导主动承担责任、早日落地、积极寻求反馈，并建立可验证的成长记录来加速职业发展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/el-nino-is-already-flooding-californias-coasts-and-the-years-highest-tides-havent-arrived-2000832574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>El Niño is flooding California&#x27;s coasts with the highest tides to come</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>厄尔尼诺正在以将至的最高涨潮冲击加州海岸</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 14:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50296585'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>El Niño is driving record-high tides along California’s coast, foreshadowing a winter of coastal flooding and erosion. The hotter Pacific and rising sea levels amplify the risk to infrastructure, beaches, and water systems. This underlines how climate variability is reshaping regional risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>厄尔尼诺正在推动加州海岸的记录性涨潮，预示着今冬可能出现海岸洪水与侵蚀。更热的太平洋气候和上升的海平面放大了对基础设施、海滩和水资源系统的风险。这凸显了气候变率正在重塑区域风险管理的现实。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何拍出黑洞照片？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 02:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50301717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50301717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50301717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article explains how scientists combine multiple telescopes via very-long-baseline interferometry to image a black hole, revealing the practical challenges and triumphs of rendering such a silhouette. It also ties the feat to broader questions about observability, data processing, and the limits of resolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释科学家如何通过甚远基线干涉法将多台望远镜联合起来成像黑洞，揭示实现轮廓所面临的实际挑战与成就。内容还把这一壮举与观测性、数据处理及分辨率的极限联系起来。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50242688'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ZX81 jokes book collects humor from the early home-computer era, highlighting retro-tech culture and shared community jokes. It shows how nostalgia drives interest in vintage hardware and storytelling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ZX81 笑话集汇集了早期家用计算机时代的幽默，捕捉了复古科技文化的趣味与自嘲。它体现了怀旧对爱好者社区的长期吸引力，以及对老派硬件叙事的推动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api.spelk.press/static/1792456337828-81-jokes-for-the-zx81-cover.webp' data-full='https://api.spelk.press/static/1792456337828-81-jokes-for-the-zx81-cover.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50292727' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fig.blue/posts/duet-is-the-future/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Duet is the first personal-assistant-type harness that feels useful</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Duet 是第一款有用的个人助手型 harness</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 10:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50292727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50292727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50292727'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Duet presents a personal-assistant-style harness that feels genuinely useful, enabling two AI assistants to collaborate on tasks with shared context. The approach demonstrates how orchestrating specialized agents can improve productivity and reduce friction for users. It points toward more practical, assistant-as-a-service tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Duet 提供了一种“个人助理式”协调框架，使两位 AI 助手协作完成任务，用户感受上更有用。该方法展示多代理协作能降低操作摩擦、提升生产力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.magnopus.com/blog/gaussiansplats-part-3-theres-always-a-bigger-scene' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gaussian Splats part 3: There&#x27;s always a bigger scene</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 07:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50209269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50209269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50209269'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The third installment examines scaling Gaussian splats for rendering complex scenes, addressing performance and memory trade-offs in representing fine details. It highlights how this approach enables more scalable, expansive visuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第三部探讨将高斯点渲染扩展到复杂场景的规模化，聚焦于在呈现细节时的性能与内存权衡。展示了此方法在实现更大场景视觉效果上的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The first wired XR glasses powered by Android XR are unveiled, integrating Google&#x27;s platform with an AR form factor. By tying XR hardware to Android XR, developers gain easier access to a broad ecosystem and consistent tooling.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.benjoffe.com/fast-floordiv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new faster algorithm to compute 64-bit signed floor division</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种更快的64位带符号向下取整除法算法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 02:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50256409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50256409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50256409'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The algorithm reduces the cost of 64-bit signed floor division, enabling faster numeric code. Benchmarks show speedups on typical CPUs, with broad implications for performance-critical software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种更快的64位带符号向下取整除法算法，降低计算成本。基准测试显示在常用 CPU 上有显著提速，对对性能敏感的软件有帮助。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mottopanikeiku/doomformer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I Turned DOOM into a Transformer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：把 DOOM 变成了 Transformer</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 16:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50297697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game. It’s a compact, proof-of-concept that sparks ideas about cross-domain AI experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。这个小型的实现是跨领域 AI 实验的一个有趣原型，值得关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.designboom.com/technology/tree-mounted-machines-web-app-users-knock-on-wood-remotely/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tree-mounted machines connect to web app to let users knock on wood remotely</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安装在树上的机器连接网页应用，让用户远程敲木头</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 06:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50258496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50258496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50258496'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>“Knock on wood” is an internet-connected device that lets users perform the superstition remotely when no wood is nearby. The device uses a web app to trigger a tree-mounted machine that knocks on wood.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“Knock on wood”是一种联网设备，让身边没有木头的用户也能远程完成这一迷信动作。用户通过网页应用触发安装在树上的机器敲击木头。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.davidpriver.com/Iterating-backwards-in-C.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iterating Backwards in C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 语言的向后迭代</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 12:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50249598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50249598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50249598'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Practical patterns for traversing arrays backwards in C, with pointer arithmetic and loop constructs that minimize off-by-one errors. It covers safety considerations, performance implications, and common pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给出在 C 语言中向后遍历数组的实用模式，包含指针运算与循环结构，帮助避免越界和边界错误。讨论安全性、性能及常见陷阱。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50221394' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50221394' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.hardill.me.uk/2026/10/24/the-curse-of-soldered-ram/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Curse of Soldered RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>焊接 RAM 的诅咒</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50221394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50221394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50221394'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Soldered RAM locks users into devices and makes upgrades impractical, raising repairability and sustainability concerns. The piece argues for modular, serviceable hardware to extend device lifespans and reduce e-waste.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>焊接 RAM 让用户无法升级，提升了维修难度并削弱了可持续性。文章主张提高硬件的可维修性和模块化设计，以延长设备寿命、降低电子垃圾。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.hardill.me.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/PXL_20261023_211704140.MP_-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://blog.hardill.me.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/PXL_20261023_211704140.MP_-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50219275' data-tags='Culture,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50219275' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brennan.day/a-love-letter-to-the-super-dimension-fortress/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Love Letter to the Super Dimension Fortress</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致敬《超时空要塞》</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 01:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50219275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nostalgic tribute to a classic sci‑fi series and its influence on design and technology culture.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50250937' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kuow.org/technology/2026-10-26/microsoft-offers-voluntary-buyouts-to-a-quarter-of-its-workforce' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft offers voluntary buyouts to a quarter of its workforce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 14:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50250937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50250937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50250937'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to about 25% of its workforce as part of an efficiency push. The program could reshape teams across cloud, AI, and consumer products and influence hiring and retention in the near term. It signals ongoing workforce optimization at a major tech company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软宣布向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，作为提升效率的一部分。该计划可能重塑云、AI 与消费品等业务线的团队结构，并影响短期内的招聘与留任。此举反映出科技巨头持续进行人力资源优化的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/95db7f3/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4032x2117+0+454/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Flegacy%2Fstore%2Ff03324410ae9bf85061382f7c6777294.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/95db7f3/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4032x2117+0+454/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Flegacy%2Fstore%2Ff03324410ae9bf85061382f7c6777294.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atgreen.github.io/repl-yell/posts/openldk-java-in-common-lisp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenLDK: Bringing the Java World into Common Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenLDK：把 Java 世界带进 Common Lisp</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 07:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50232101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50232101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50232101'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenLDK aims to bridge Java and Common Lisp, enabling Lisp programs to call Java libraries and run on the Java platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenLDK 旨在把 Java 世界引入 Common Lisp，使 Lisp 程序能够调用 Java 库并运行在 Java 平台上。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50296947' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50296947' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claremontreviewofbooks.com/the-great-american-rascal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Great American Rascal: Tom Sawyer at 150</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国伟大顽童：汤姆·索亚150周年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 15:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50296947'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On its 150th anniversary, Tom Sawyer is re-examined as a cornerstone of American literature and cultural myth. The piece traces Twain’s mischievous boy as a vehicle for debates about childhood freedom, social norms, and American identity, and surveys his lasting influence on readers and media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在汤姆·索亚诞辰150周年之际，该作被重新解读为美国文学与文化神话的重要基石。文章追溯马克·吐温笔下这个顽皮男孩如何推动关于童年自由、社会规范与美国身份的讨论，并考察他对后世文学与大众文化的持续影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claremontreviewofbooks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/CRB_FA26_Wolfson-573x400.png' data-full='https://claremontreviewofbooks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/CRB_FA26_Wolfson-573x400.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50196623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50196623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50289590' data-tags='AI,Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50289590' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/owls-on-wires/keplearn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Keplearn: Extract Equations from Data in Seconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Keplearn：秒级从数据中提取方程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 07:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50289590'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems. This accelerates scientific experimentation and can complement traditional regression with interpretable models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。这加速了科学实验，并可与传统回归方法结合，产生可解释的模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f1fe2c15f1daa158a9068afde3b380bd2af8eaca264687d4476957b0553b02e8/owls-on-wires/keplearn' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f1fe2c15f1daa158a9068afde3b380bd2af8eaca264687d4476957b0553b02e8/owls-on-wires/keplearn' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dmux.github.io/minilake/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MiniLake Is a Local Databricks API Emulator with SQL, Delta Lake and Spark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MiniLake：本地 Databricks API 仿真器，支持 SQL、Delta Lake 与 Spark</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 07:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50259697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50259697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50259697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MiniLake is a local Databricks API emulator providing SQL, Delta Lake, and Spark compatibility, enabling developers to test Databricks workflows offline or locally. It helps teams iron out integration issues early and accelerates development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MiniLake 是一个本地化的 Databricks API 仿真器，提供 SQL、Delta Lake 与 Spark 兼容性，使开发者能够离线或本地测试 Databricks 工作流。它帮助团队尽早发现集成问题，加速开发。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50316760' data-tags='Legal,AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50316760' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/ex-meta-researcher-torrented-pirated-books-dataset-deleted-it-due-to-all-this-litigation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ex-Meta Researcher Torrented Pirated Books, Deleted It &#x27;Due to Litigation&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前 Meta 研究员曾通过种子下载盗版书籍，称因诉讼而删除</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 07:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50316760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50316760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50316760'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former Meta researcher testified that they downloaded an AI training dataset containing the pirated Books3 collection through BitTorrent. Meta is facing several California federal lawsuits over the use of pirated books to train its Llama models. The cases are separate but are being handled together by Judge Vince Chhabria. Plaintiffs include authors Sarah Silverman and Ta-Nehisi Coates, as well as several publishers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名前 Meta 研究员作证称，自己曾通过 BitTorrent 下载包含盗版 Books3 书籍集合的 AI 训练数据集。Meta 正因使用盗版书籍训练 Llama 模型而在加州联邦法院面临多起诉讼。这些案件彼此独立，但由法官 Vince Chhabria 统一审理。原告包括 Sarah Silverman、Ta-Nehisi Coates 等作者及多家出版商。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://torrentfreak.com/images/books-long.png' data-full='https://torrentfreak.com/images/books-long.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50220331' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50220331' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hallofdreams.org/posts/physicsforums/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PhysicsForums and the Dead Internet Theory (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PhysicsForums 与‘死网理论’（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 04:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50220331'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It likely investigates how communities withstand misinformation and what the analogy means for science forums and community moderation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文将 PhysicsForums 与‘死网理论’联系起来，评估关于论坛内容被机器人主导、以及网络话语质量下降的说法。讨论聚焦科学社区的信息真实性、版规治理，以及这类论调对科普交流的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50294770' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patrick Stewart Has Died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 12:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50294770'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patrick Stewart, the iconic actor known for his Star Trek captain and stage work, has died. His career spanned theater, film, and television, shaping pop culture across decades. His legacy includes leadership in science fiction media and mentorship of performers worldwide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕特里克·斯图尔特这位因科幻角色和舞台表演而闻名的演员去世，职业生涯跨越舞台、电视和电影，对文化影响深远。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50285714' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50285714' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.timeanddate.com/news/time/cuba-keeps-dst-until-nov-2027.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cuba is skipping its November 1, 2026 clock change and staying on DST until 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>古巴将继续使用夏令时，直至 2027 年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 00:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50285714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50285714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50285714'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cuba has decided to skip the clock change on November 1, 2026 and will stay on DST through 2027, affecting scheduling and cross-border coordination with neighboring regions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古巴决定在 2026 年 11 月 1 日跳过时钟调整，夏令时将延续至 2027 年。这一决策将影响日常安排和与周边地区的跨境协作节奏。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50267569' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50267569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/maharmstone/moxnox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moxnox 1.0: NT-Like, POSIX-Compatible OS Running Atop Btrfs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moxnox 1.0：在 Btrfs 上的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容操作系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 16:53 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 Moxnox 1.0，这是一个在 Btrfs 文件系统上实现的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容的操作系统原型。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50308986' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50308986' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/11/02/a-pickpockets-guide-to-new-york' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Pickpocket&#x27;s Guide to New York</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《纽约扒手指南》</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 12:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50308986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50308986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50308986'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>To prepare for playing a petty thief in a new film, John Turturro learned pickpocketing techniques from consultant Apollo Robbins. Turturro plays Harry, an old-school thief who accidentally steals a flash drive from the wrong person and angers the Mob. Robbins, known as “the gentleman thief,” has performed staged thefts for audiences and once emptied the pockets of members of Jimmy Carter’s Secret Service detail. The story compares pickpocketing with acting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为准备在新电影中饰演小偷，约翰·特托罗向顾问阿波罗·罗宾斯学习扒窃技巧。特托罗饰演老派小偷哈里，他意外从错误对象身上偷走闪存盘，因而激怒黑帮。被称为“绅士小偷”的罗宾斯曾为观众表演模拟盗窃，还曾清空吉米·卡特特勤团队成员的口袋。文章将扒窃与表演进行了比较。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6adbdbae242185e214d1ae89/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/r50580.png' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6adbdbae242185e214d1ae89/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/r50580.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50293023' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50293023' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/world/europe/karl-bushby-walking-globe-uk.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>After 28 years walking across the globe, he&#x27;s almost home</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>环游全球28年后，他几乎回到家</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 10:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50293023'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After nearly three decades of walking, Karl Bushby is nearing the end of his land-based circumnavigation, tackling remaining miles and bureaucratic hurdles. The feat underscores the perseverance and logistical challenges of long-running exploration, and highlights how cross-border routes complicate modern adventures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>几乎三十年的行走旅程接近尾声，Karl Bushby 即将抵达家的门槛，同时还要应对剩余路途与繁琐的边界与许可问题。这一壮举凸显了持久探索的毅力以及跨境路线对现代冒险的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50255177'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal post about Pratt parsing serves as a memory aid; Pratt parsing is a practical operator-precedence technique used to implement expression grammars. The notes share mental models and coding tips that help readers build compact, readable parsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博主写下关于 Pratt 解析法的笔记，以帮助记忆并厘清思路；Pratt 解析是一种处理运算符优先级和结合性的自顶向下解析方法。文中分享了直观模型与实现要点，对实现紧凑而易读的解析器很有帮助。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50291116' data-tags='AI,Startups,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50291116' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Simantic (YC F26) – Firmware Simulation for AI Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Simantic（YC F26）— 面向 AI 代理的固件仿真</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 08:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50291116'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Simantic provides firmware-level simulation tooling to test AI agents in realistic hardware-like environments, enabling safer experimentation and faster iteration without physical prototypes. By integrating with existing agent runtimes, it helps developers reason about latency, memory, and real-world constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Simantic 提供用于 AI 代理的固件级仿真工具，帮助在接近真实硬件的环境中进行安全测试和快速迭代，避免依赖实际硬件原型。通过与现有代理运行时集成，便于开发者评估延迟、内存与实世界约束。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50278645' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50278645' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/theconsensuslabs/noalloc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>noalloc: Tooling and libraries to enforce zero heap allocations in Golang</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>noalloc：在 Golang 中实现零堆分配的工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 11:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50278645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50278645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50278645'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services. It helps developers reason about memory use, though it may constrain certain idioms and require careful design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。它帮助开发者管理内存使用，但可能限制某些编码习惯并需谨慎设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e09ab6b29c292c59030739495c47357f59b027b500db18119d200acd099708fe/theconsensuslabs/noalloc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e09ab6b29c292c59030739495c47357f59b027b500db18119d200acd099708fe/theconsensuslabs/noalloc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50235316'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>William Gibson&#x27;s Agency project teases a new piece titled &#x27;Eunice is almost here&#x27;, signaling another entry in his near-future fiction. The teaser hints at Gibson&#x27;s ongoing exploration of networked culture, AI, and the social implications of technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>威廉·吉布森的 Agency 项目预告了题为《Eunice is almost here》的作品，预示他将推出又一部近未来题材的小说。预告暗示他将继续探讨网络文化、人工智能及科技的社会影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50196212' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50196212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/researchers-identify-new-circadian-subtype-of-depression-affecting-1-in-4-patients' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New &#x27;Circadian&#x27; Subtype of Depression Affecting 1 in 4 Patients</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型昼夜节律抑郁症，影响约四分之一患者</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 22, 2026 / 07:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50196212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50196212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50196212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers identify a circadian subtype of depression affecting about 25% of patients, linked to disruptions in daily rhythms. This points to timing-aware treatments and chronotherapy as potential avenues for diagnosis and management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员发现一种新的昼夜节律型抑郁症亚型，约影响四分之一患者，与日常节律紊乱相关。这为以时间为导向的治疗和昼夜节律治疗提供了潜在的新方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/GettyImages-2280049531-2560x1440-1.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/GettyImages-2280049531-2560x1440-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 10:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based tool that lets presenters show slides side-by-side with speaker notes, without sharing their entire screen. It can improve privacy and reduce streaming bandwidth during remote meetings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个基于浏览器的工具，允许演讲者与讲者备注并排展示幻灯片，而无需共享整个屏幕。提升隐私，并在远程会议中降低流式带宽需求。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50242509'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Java&#x27;s AOT caching feature is still rough around the edges, with questions about stability, performance gains, and portability across runtimes. The post suggests careful evaluation before adoption in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Java 的 AOT 缓存特性仍不稳定，在稳定性、性能收益和跨运行时的可移植性等方面存在疑问。建议在生产环境中审慎评估再使用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50246538' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50246538' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenumb.at/Syl-Intro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compile-Time Dependent Types</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编译期依赖类型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 09:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50246538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50246538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50246538'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The project argues that compile-time dependent types are likely to reach systems programming. It compares existing mechanisms including C++ templates and constexpr, Rust const generics, Zig comptime, and C _Generic, while noting that they are restricted or lack a full type system. The author is developing Syl, a language intended to encode compile-time computation in a modal type system. Syl is inspired by two-level type theory and plans for OxCaml.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目认为，编译期依赖类型最终很可能进入系统编程领域。文章比较了 C++ 模板和 constexpr、Rust const generics、Zig comptime 以及 C 的 _Generic，同时指出这些机制存在限制或缺乏完整类型系统。作者正在开发 Syl，目标是在模态类型系统中编码编译期计算。Syl 的灵感来自两层类型论和 OxCaml 的规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thenumb.at/icon.jpg' data-full='https://thenumb.at/icon.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-spiders-spin-silk-dope-into-natures-toughest-material-20261028/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spiders Spin &#x27;Silk Dope&#x27; into Nature&#x27;s Toughest Material</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜘蛛把丝胶旋成自然界最坚韧的材料</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 10:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50277755'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reveal the process by which spiders spin silk dope into a remarkably strong material, shedding light on protein chemistry and the dynamics of the spinning process. The findings could inspire new biomimetic materials for tech and industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员揭示蜘蛛如何把丝胶旋成极其坚韧的自然材料，揭示蛋白质化学与旋丝过程的机理。这些发现可能为仿生材料在科技与工业应用中带来新思路。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sylphide OS presents a general-purpose operating system designed for microcontroller-based computers, potentially bridging embedded devices with more traditional OS concepts. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sylphide OS 为基于微控制器的计算机提供通用操作系统，试图将嵌入式设备与传统操作系统概念连接起来。该项目在模块化、调度和驱动支持方面探索，为更强大的嵌入式应用打开了新可能。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50262982' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50262982' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.vt.edu/articles/2026/10/cmi-monarchs.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Radio-tagged Monarch butterflies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带无线标签的君主蝶迁徙研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 10:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50262982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50262982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50262982'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists attach radio tags to monarch butterflies to track their migration paths across continents. The data illuminate routes, stopover habitats, and threats, informing conservation strategies for this iconic species.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员给君主蝶安装无线标签，以追踪其跨大洲的迁徙路径。数据揭示迁徙路线、停栖栖息地与威胁情况，为保护这一标志性物种提供科学依据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.vt.edu/content/dam/news_vt_edu/articles/2026/10/cnre/CMI-butterfly-tagging-2.jpg.transform/l-medium/image.jpg' data-full='https://news.vt.edu/content/dam/news_vt_edu/articles/2026/10/cnre/CMI-butterfly-tagging-2.jpg.transform/l-medium/image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sonidoselva.com/perspectiva-kogui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Urgent Message on Artificial Intelligence from the Kogui People</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科吉族对人工智能的紧急呼吁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 04:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50287359'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Kogui people issue an urgent message about AI, urging ethical use, data sovereignty, and protection of indigenous knowledge. The statement calls on policymakers and technologists to center consent, cultural rights, and land stewardship in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科吉族发出关于人工智能的紧急呼声，呼吁在 AI 应用中遵循伦理、数据主权与对本土知识的保护。声明敦促政策制定者与技术人员将同意权、文化权利与土地管理置于 AI 发展的核心。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img1.wsimg.com/isteam/ip/93fe72a0-3f74-44e1-82b7-5cd9b2c3bce5/SS%20Logo%20White.png' data-full='https://img1.wsimg.com/isteam/ip/93fe72a0-3f74-44e1-82b7-5cd9b2c3bce5/SS%20Logo%20White.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video of VCF Southwest 2026 is edited to resemble Silicon Graphics-era broadcast on a 1990s TV. The retro aesthetic juxtaposes modern conference content with vintage hardware flair.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把 VCF Southwest 2026 的视频编辑成 Silicon Graphics 时代的电视播出风格，呈现1990年代的怀旧观感。这样的复古美学将现代科技议题与老式硬件气质并置。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50263701'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three TLS bugs were found in GoAhead, a compact embedded web server. The post explains the flaws, their impact on security, and how to patch or mitigate them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 GoAhead 这款嵌入式 Web 服务器中发现了三处 TLS 漏洞，可能影响加密通信的安全性。文中给出漏洞影响、修补与缓解建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276355' data-tags='Career,Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50276355' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ideolalia.com/essays/trapped-in-the-technologist-factory.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trapped in the Technologist Factory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>困在技术专家工厂里</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 09:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author recalls being paid to use Clojure at an early-stage startup two years after the language was released. The startup, Runa, offered dynamic pricing for small online retailers, but in practice gave discounts to customers who abandoned shopping carts while keeping a control group. Its main challenges were persuading retailers to let it control prices and convincing them that its measured sales impact was sound.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回忆说，在 Clojure 发布两年后，自己曾在一家早期创业公司因使用该语言获得报酬。这家名为 Runa 的公司原计划为小型在线零售商提供动态定价，但实际做法是向放弃购物车的顾客提供固定折扣，同时保留对照组。公司面临的主要困难，是说服零售商允许其控制价格，并让对方相信测得的销售提升确实可靠。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50298318'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A digital library offering access to Cormac McCarthy&#x27;s works and related manuscripts, with organized collections and metadata for researchers and readers. It provides a centralized, searchable resource for studying the author’s catalog.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供科马克·麦卡锡作品及相关手稿的数字图书馆，配有有序的藏书与元数据，方便研究者与读者检索与研究。形成研究者可集中使用的资源。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50271521' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamanetwork.com/journals/jamanetworkopen/fullarticle/2855858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Study: Schoolwide phone bans show no significant impact on mental health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究：校园手机禁令对心理健康无显著影响</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 03:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50271521'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study across schools finds that broad phone bans do not measurably improve student mental health, challenging assumptions that device removal alone boosts well-being. It suggests that policy should consider broader support structures and context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《JAMA Network Open》的一项研究显示，学校范围的手机禁令对学生心理健康并无显著改善，质疑无设备政策本身就有益的假设。研究强调需要综合考虑心理健康支持与校园文化等因素。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50233347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A mathematician offers a rigorous lens on AI&#x27;s trajectory, exploring how mathematical thinking informs reliability, scalability, and the long-term societal implications of AI. The piece emphasizes foundational thinking in shaping the future.</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 22:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50255161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50255161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based SQLite editor offers instant experimentation without local installs, enabling quick schema edits, queries, and prototyping. It also raises security considerations for client-side data handling and offline use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于浏览器的 SQLite 编辑器让开发者无需本地安装即可快速修改模式、执行查询和进行原型设计。同时也需关注客户端数据处理的安全性与离线使用的风险。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 05:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50231041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50231041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50231041'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tour of 64K Lisp demonstrates compact, self-contained Lisp systems—tiny interpreters, macros, and mini compilers designed to fit in limited RAM. The piece celebrates Lisp&#x27;s expressive power and the fun of code golf-style constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>64K 核心内存中的 LISPy 技巧展示了在极小内存下的 Lisp 系统设计：紧凑的解释器、宏和小型编译器等。文章庆祝 Lisp 的表达力与在代码极简约约束下的乐趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://t3x.org/lisp64k/toroids-x50-s.jpg' data-full='https://t3x.org/lisp64k/toroids-x50-s.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50314872' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50314872' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shiny_object_syndrome' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shiny Object Syndrome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>闪亮对象综合征</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 04:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50314872'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shiny object syndrome describes focusing excessive attention on a new and fashionable idea, then abandoning it when another novelty appears. The concept treats such fixation as a distraction that can cloud judgment and weaken focus. People who fear missing out may be especially susceptible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“闪亮对象综合征”指人们过度关注新颖、流行的想法，却在出现下一个新事物后将其完全抛弃。这一概念将这种执着视为会干扰判断、削弱专注力的分心因素。害怕错过机会的人可能尤其容易受到影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' data-full='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50305724' data-tags='AI,Culture,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50305724' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alanbellows.com/some-thing-is-happening/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Some Thing is Happening A note from the AI companies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>某件事正在发生：AI 公司的一封信</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 08:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50305724'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The satirical note is addressed to creative workers by the makers of large language models. It says the companies built large computers to scan the internet, books, magazines, and archives into collections of creative works. It then says they intend to profit from unlimited, uncredited, and uncompensated imitations of that work. The note frames this as a critique of how AI companies use creative material.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这封讽刺性公开信以大语言模型制造商的口吻写给创作者。信中称，这些公司建造了大型计算机，扫描互联网、书籍、杂志和档案，汇集创作作品。随后它宣称，公司打算从这些作品的无限、无署名且无偿的模仿中获利。这封信借此批评 AI 公司使用创作内容的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alanbellows.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/P7010055-1024x768.jpg' data-full='https://alanbellows.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/P7010055-1024x768.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/destined/hurston-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Hurston-1 open source TTS that speak Southern American and Afro-English</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Hurston-1 开源 TTS 可说南方美式英语与 Afro-English</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 18:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50227330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50227330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50227330'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hurston-1 is an open-source text-to-speech model that adds voices for Southern American and Afro-English dialects, expanding dialect representation in TTS and accessibility for diverse speakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hurston-1 是一个开源文本转语音模型，新增南方美式英语和 Afro-English 方言声音，扩展了 TTS 的方言表示和对多样语音使用者的可及性。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50234699' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vithushansylvester.com/writing/the-real-impact-of-ai-on-tech-jobs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI didn&#x27;t cause the tech jobs bust, but it explains why there&#x27;s no recovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 不是造成科技岗位下滑的原因，但解释了为何尚无复苏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 11:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50234699'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI didn’t cause the tech jobs bust, but it explains why there’s no immediate rebound—structural labor-market factors, long retraining cycles, and investment lags slow hiring in tech. The piece advocates targeted upskilling and supportive policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 并非导致科技岗位下滑的直接原因，但解释了为何短期内尚无复苏——劳动力市场的结构性因素、再培训周期长、投资回暖滞后等在科技领域放慢了招聘。文中倡导有针对性的技能提升和政策支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vithushansylvester.com/writing/the-real-impact-of-ai-on-tech-jobs/opengraph-image?66fbde5ab7d04a0f' data-full='https://vithushansylvester.com/writing/the-real-impact-of-ai-on-tech-jobs/opengraph-image?66fbde5ab7d04a0f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50317429' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50317429' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://science.nasa.gov/mission/voyager/voyager-1/voyager-1-what-is-a-light-day/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is a light-day? The milestone Voyager 1 is closing in on</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么是光日？旅行者 1 号正在接近的里程碑</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 09:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50317429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Voyager 1 is approaching the milestone of being one light-day from Earth, a distance rather than a measure of time. One light-day is about 16 billion miles, or 26 billion kilometers. The spacecraft, launched nearly 50 years ago, is expected to reach that point on November 18, 2026, at 5:16:07 a.m. EST.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>旅行者 1 号正在接近距地球一光日这一里程碑；光日表示距离，而不是时间单位。一光日约为 160 亿英里，即 260 亿公里。这艘已飞行近 50 年的航天器预计将在 2026 年 11 月 18 日美国东部时间上午 5:16:07 到达该位置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.science.nasa.gov/content/dam/science/psd/photojournal/pia/pia21/pia21839/PIA21839.tif/jcr:content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' data-full='https://assets.science.nasa.gov/content/dam/science/psd/photojournal/pia/pia21/pia21839/PIA21839.tif/jcr:content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
