<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50298721' data-tags='Space,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50298721' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://science.nasa.gov/blog/tracks-along-the-longest-drive-on-another-world/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracks along the longest drive on another world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在另一颗星球上的最长行驶记录</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 18:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50298721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50298721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50298721'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA&#x27;s update covers the record longest drive by a planetary rover on another world, detailing the route, terrain challenges, and what scientists hope to learn from the extended trek. It highlights engineering feats that enable longer exploration in space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 博文记录另一颗星球上探测车的最长驱动里程，介绍行驶路线、地形挑战以及科学家希望从这段长途探测中获得的知识，凸显支撑长距离探测的工程成就。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/the-tragedy-of-losing-your-wikipedia-page/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The tragedy of losing your Wikipedia page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丢失维基百科条目的悲剧</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 10:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50247342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50247342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50247342'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece uses cases of Wikipedia pages being deleted or falling off the public radar to illustrate how digital identity is curated by notability rules. It highlights the fragility of online notability and how researchers and readers rely on stable biographical pages for context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章用维基百科页面被删除或消失的案例，揭示数字身份如何受到“可见性”规则的塑造。此类不稳定性让研究者和读者在寻求背景信息时面临挑战，也引发对在线传记永久性的反思。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ada50d1133035a6bb08be2d/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/WIRED-Ghost-Stories-Wikipedia-Notable-top.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ada50d1133035a6bb08be2d/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/WIRED-Ghost-Stories-Wikipedia-Notable-top.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/28/ai-execs-may-be-too-out-of-touch-to-build-for-the-general-public/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI execs may be too out of touch to build for the general public</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50281826'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 AI 高管可能与普通用户脱节，难以打造面向公众的实用产品。文章提倡更务实的产品驱动领导力与更密切的用户对齐。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50293437' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50293437' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/turning-a-solar-power-system-into-its-own-backup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turning a solar power system into its own backup</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让太阳能系统成为自己的备用电源</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 11:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50293437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A plant design allows concentrated solar power to run on either stored solar heat or combustion. Concentrated solar systems use mirrors to convert sunlight into heat for driving conventional turbines. Although heat storage once suggested that the technology could provide power around the clock, falling photovoltaic prices made solar panels cheaper in most locations. The new design uses combustion as an alternative to stored solar heat.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种电站设计让聚光太阳能系统既可以使用储存的太阳热能，也可以使用燃烧供能。聚光太阳能通过镜面将阳光转化为热量，再驱动传统发电涡轮机。虽然储热技术一度让其具备全天候发电的前景，但光伏价格下降后，太阳能板在多数地区更便宜。新设计将燃烧作为储存太阳热能之外的替代能源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/image-2-1152x648.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/image-2-1152x648.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.getty.edu/news/buddha-head-of-merv-turkmenistan-conservation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making the Buddha Head from Merv Whole Again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让梅尔夫的佛头重新完整</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 11:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50213610'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Conservationists restore the Buddha Head of Merv in Turkmenistan to its former glory, using modern restoration techniques to stabilize cracks and reassemble missing fragments. The project highlights the challenges of preserving ancient artifacts in changing climates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自土库曼斯坦梅尔夫的佛头修复工作让其完整如初，采用现代修复技术修补裂缝与缺失碎片。此举凸显在气候变迁背景下保护古代文物的挑战与意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.getty.edu/iiif/image/55089276-2fea-4c43-ab96-27a676a6fae4/0,251,498,261/498,261/0/default.jpg' data-full='https://media.getty.edu/iiif/image/55089276-2fea-4c43-ab96-27a676a6fae4/0,251,498,261/498,261/0/default.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.raspberrypi.com/news/recreate-the-apollo-guidance-computer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recreate the Apollo Guidance Computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>重现阿波罗导航计算机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 22, 2026 / 07:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50195769'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raspberry Pi project recreates the Apollo Guidance Computer, offering hands-on insight into vintage avionics and the engineering behind the Apollo missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>树莓派团队推出重现阿波罗导航计算机的项目，为公众提供对古董航空电子设备和阿波罗任务背后工程的动手了解。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50242873' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50242873' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-026-78272-8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human-enhanced tsunami hazard in large lakes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型湖泊中的人为放大海啸风险</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 06:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50242873'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Human activities are shown to amplify tsunami hazards in large lakes, with dam operations and shoreline modifications increasing wave amplification. The finding broadens risk assessments for communities around big lakes and informs mitigation planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明人类活动会加剧大型湖泊的海啸风险，水坝运行和岸线改造等因素放大波浪效应。该发现有助于扩展对湖泊周边社区的风险评估与防灾规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-026-78272-8/MediaObjects/41467_2026_78272_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-026-78272-8/MediaObjects/41467_2026_78272_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276613' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50276613' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bellone.com/technical/double-buffering-with-cloudflare-queues' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Double Buffering with Cloudflare Queues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Cloudflare Queues 实现双缓冲</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 09:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276613'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The telemetry pipeline uses a double-buffered queue to collect and segment events into groups bounded by UTC hours. Cloudflare Workers, Queues, Workflows, and R2 handle capture, batching, processing, and storage. Modulo arithmetic routes payloads to the active queue and drains the inactive one, while UTC avoids daylight-saving-time complications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该遥测管道使用双缓冲队列收集事件，并按 UTC 小时分组切分。Cloudflare Workers、Queues、Workflows 和 R2 分别参与捕获、批处理、处理和存储。系统利用取模运算将负载路由到活动队列，并排空非活动队列，同时使用 UTC 避免夏令时处理问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bellone.com/static/images/b_icon_white.svg' data-full='https://bellone.com/static/images/b_icon_white.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50271457' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50271457' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/barcelona-unrest-how-to-fabricate-a-protest-in-the-age-of-tiktok-295263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Barcelona unrest: how to fabricate a protest in the age of TikTok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴塞罗那骚乱：TikTok 时代如何制造示威</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 03:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50271457'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It discusses indicators and guardrails for journalists and platforms to mitigate inauthentic activity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章检视短视频平台如何被用来制造或放大抗议叙事，凸显在实时事件中的信息误导、协同与真实性问题。讨论了记者与平台可采取的监测要点与治理边界，以抑制不真实行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/768470/original/file-20261027-52-bez8xb.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C125%2C4000%2C2000&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/768470/original/file-20261027-52-bez8xb.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C125%2C4000%2C2000&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50250609' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50250609' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hbrooks.com/writing/image-generation-has-come-a-long-way' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Image generation has come a long way since August 2022</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自 2022 年 8 月以来，图像生成技术已取得长足进步</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 13:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50250609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50250609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50250609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Image generation has progressed significantly since August 2022, with advances in realism, speed, safety, and multimodal capabilities. The piece traces the evolution across models, tooling, and consumer-facing applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自 2022 年 8 月以来，图像生成技术取得了显著进步，体现在真实感、速度、安全性和多模态能力上。文章梳理了在模型、工具链和面向大众的应用方面的演进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hbrooks.com/_next/image?url=%2Fmedia%2Fdalle2%2F01-1.jpg&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75' data-full='https://www.hbrooks.com/_next/image?url=%2Fmedia%2Fdalle2%2F01-1.jpg&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50314243' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50314243' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.osnews.com/story/146273/how-long-do-you-need-to-hold-down-the-power-button-to-turn-off-a-pc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How long do you need to hold down the power button to turn off a PC?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>要按住电源键多久才能关闭电脑？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 01:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50314243'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Holding a PC’s power button for 12 seconds turns it off at the hardware level without notifying the operating system. This is a safety feature intended to work even when the operating system’s normal shutdown command is not functioning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>按住电脑电源键12秒，会在硬件层面关闭电脑，而不会通知操作系统。这是一项安全功能，即使操作系统的正常关机命令失效，也能用来关闭电脑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50245945' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50245945' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stalw.art/blog/stalwart-1-0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stalwart v1.0: The Fastest Mail Server on the Planet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stalwart v1.0：地球上最快的邮件服务器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 08:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50245945'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stalwart v1.0 claims record-breaking mail throughput, emphasizing architectural choices like asynchronous I/O and efficient stores. The notes discuss deployment considerations for high-traffic domains and potential market impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stalwart v1.0 声称创造了前所未有的邮件吞吐量，强调异步 I/O 与高效存储等架构要点。文中探讨在高流量域名下的部署要点与对市场的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stalw.art/og/blog/stalwart-1-0.png' data-full='https://stalw.art/og/blog/stalwart-1-0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50211780' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://petapixel.com/2026/10/22/sigma-says-the-compact-modular-fp2-is-the-ultimate-hybrid-camera/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sigma Says the Compact, Modular FP2 Is the Ultimate Hybrid Camera</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sigma 称紧凑、模块化 FP2 是终极混合相机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 09:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50211780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50211780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50211780'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sigma pitches the FP2 as a modular hybrid camera blending stills, video, and computational features in a compact form. The approach mirrors a broader trend toward lightweight, configurable cameras for creators and professionals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sigma 将 FP2 描述为紧凑、模块化的混合相机，集静态拍摄、视频与计算功能于一体。该设计体现了面向创作者与专业人士的轻量化、可配置相机趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://petapixel.com/assets/uploads/2026/10/sigma-fp2-featured.jpg' data-full='https://petapixel.com/assets/uploads/2026/10/sigma-fp2-featured.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50239027' data-tags='Politics,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50239027' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://contraptions.venkateshrao.com/p/cognitive-mercantilism' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cognitive Mercantilism: AI panic resembles fears of early capitalism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>认知重商主义：AI恐慌与早期资本主义担忧的相似性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 21:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50239027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50239027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50239027'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cognitive Mercantilism argues that AI panic mirrors fears from the early days of capitalism, with power coalescing around data and platforms. It analyzes incentives, governance, and strategic behavior that amplify the discourse. The piece suggests we should study historical patterns to design better AI policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认知重商主义认为，AI的恐慌类似于资本主义早期的担忧，数据与平台的掌控权成为核心焦点。文章剖析推动这种话语的激励、治理结构及策略行为，并强调用历史规律来设计更明智的AI政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0-U5!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fcafd3438-ef0d-41c0-83cf-bcc7e1c995be_1536x1024.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0-U5!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fcafd3438-ef0d-41c0-83cf-bcc7e1c995be_1536x1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50227041' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adrs.systems' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: ADRs for AI – Deliberation records for human–AI work, versioned in Git</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的 ADRs——用于人机协作的 deliberation 记录，版本化在 Git</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 24, 2026 / 17:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50227041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50227041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50227041'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ADRs for AI introduces versioned deliberation records to journal human–AI collaboration, stored in Git. They aim to improve traceability of AI-assisted decisions and rationale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ADRs for AI 提出了用于记录人机协作推理过程的决策记录，版本化并保存在 Git 中，以提升对AI辅助决策的追溯性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nedbatchelder.com/blog/201711/toxic_experts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Toxic experts (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2017 年的有毒专家</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 09:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50291927'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Toxic experts (2017) examines how certain expert communities can harbor counterproductive dynamics, offering lessons on governance, collaboration, and the psychology of expertise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2017 年的“有毒的专家”一文探讨了某些专家群体内部可能存在的破坏性动态，提出治理、协作与专业认知心理学的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/full/10.1145/3706598.3713778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Impact of Generative AI on Critical Thinking: Self-Reported (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生成式 AI 对批判性思维的影响</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 11:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318401'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The title identifies a discussion of generative AI’s impact on critical thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>标题指出，该内容讨论生成式 AI 对批判性思维的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50244486' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50244486' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/commodore-1600-vicmodem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Commodore 1600 VICmodem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Commodore 1600 VICmodem</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 07:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50244486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50244486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50244486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Commodore 1600 VICmodem was introduced in 1982 as an affordable home modem, with a target price below $100. Designed by Anchor Automation, it debuted at the Winter Consumer Electronics Show on January 7 and reached the market in March 1982. It was intended to let VIC-20 owners participate in the emerging information age at relatively low cost. The modem was reportedly illegal in Canada.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Commodore 1600 VICmodem 于1982年推出，是一款面向家庭市场的廉价调制解调器，目标价格低于100美元。它由 Anchor Automation 设计，于1982年1月7日在冬季消费电子展上亮相，并于当年3月上市。它旨在让 VIC-20 用户以相对低廉的成本参与早期信息时代。据称，这款调制解调器在加拿大属于非法产品。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/800px-CommodoreVICModem.jpg' data-full='https://dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/800px-CommodoreVICModem.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50271052' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50271052' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fgiesen.wordpress.com/2015/10/25/reading-and-writing-are-less-symmetric-than-you-probably-think/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reading and writing are less symmetric than you (probably) think</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>读和写并不像你想象的那样对称</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 02:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50271052'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The discussion concerns computing I/O operations, using the POSIX read and write system calls as examples. It gives their C prototypes and explains that user programs usually access them through buffered I/O layers such as the C standard library’s fread and fwrite. For ordinary storage, the distinction between reading and writing becomes important beyond the basic system-call interface.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论计算机中的输入输出操作，并以 POSIX 的 read 和 write 系统调用为例。文中给出了它们的 C 函数原型，并解释用户程序通常通过 C 标准库中的 fread 和 fwrite 等缓冲 I/O 层来使用这些功能。对于普通存储设备而言，读写之间的差异并不止于基础系统调用接口。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50213502' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50213502' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://categorica.io/blog/2026.10.16_nginx-stats-prometheus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Native Prometheus Integration with Nginx</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nginx 的原生 Prometheus 集成</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 11:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50213502'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prometheus-native metrics for Nginx simplify observability for high-traffic services. The integration may use embedded metrics endpoints, reducing external exporters and configuration burden. This enables more actionable latency and error monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nginx 的 Prometheus 原生指标集成简化了对高流量服务的观测工作。新集成可能采用内置指标端点，减少对外部导出器的依赖，并降低配置复杂度。这样可以让延迟和错误监控更加直观且可操作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://categorica.io/_app/immutable/assets/ctg_banner.DMSiAwkS.svg' data-full='https://categorica.io/_app/immutable/assets/ctg_banner.DMSiAwkS.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50291812' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50291812' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/arterialist/kube32' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I rebuilt Kubernetes for ESP32 microcontrollers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我为 ESP32 微控制器重做了 Kubernetes</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 09:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291812' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291812' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50291812'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers. The project lets kubectl and k9s manage boards over Wi-Fi or USB serial, while pods run WebAssembly modules through the wasm3 interpreter. A board with PSRAM serves as the control plane, and boards without PSRAM act as workers. The same sources also build a macOS and Linux host simulator.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。该项目支持通过 Wi-Fi 或 USB 串口使用 kubectl 和 k9s 管理开发板，并通过 wasm3 解释器运行 WebAssembly 模块。带 PSRAM 的开发板充当控制平面，不带 PSRAM 的开发板作为工作节点。同一套源代码还可构建 macOS 和 Linux 主机模拟器。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3a66984344ac6b5ba8d7d545c3d797e01c747431760d285fc85e33b25940be41/arterialist/kube32' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3a66984344ac6b5ba8d7d545c3d797e01c747431760d285fc85e33b25940be41/arterialist/kube32' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chengela.substack.com/p/should-a-medical-diagnosis-reshape' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Should a medical diagnosis reshape the past? (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>医疗诊断应否重塑过去？（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 15:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50296920'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thought-provoking piece on whether new medical information should reframe how we interpret prior symptoms and histories. It delves into the ethics of retrospective reinterpretation, memory integrity, and how this intersects with patient autonomy and data governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇引人深思的文章讨论新诊断是否应重新解读过去的症状与病史，探讨回溯性解释的伦理、记忆的完整性，以及与患者自主权和数据治理的关系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://divergencelabs.com/publications/ige-preprint.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IgE disruptor for food allergy with Claude-enabled antibody design [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 Claude 的抗体设计的 IgE 破坏剂研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 12:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50294486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A preprint describing an IgE disruptor designed with Claude-assisted antibody design, aiming to address food allergies. It demonstrates the growing role of AI in protein design and therapeutic development, while also highlighting the need for rigorous validation and safety studies. The work signals AI-accelerated biology, with risks and promise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份预印本提出利用 Claude 启动的抗体设计来干扰食品过敏相关的 IgE 通路，具有潜在治疗意义。此类工作显示了 AI 辅助蛋白设计向实际治疗靠拢的趋势，但仍需大量实验验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reverse-engineering study of a Beyblade launch tracker reveals hardware-level vulnerabilities and design choices that affect timing precision. It offers a playful example of hardware hacking techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对贝耶牌发射跟踪器的反向工程揭示了硬件层面的漏洞与设计决策如何影响计时精度，为硬件黑客技术提供有趣示例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.artnet.com/art-world/daguerre-photograph-subjects-location-2826596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>188-Year-Old Mystery of Photography&#x27;s First Human Subjects Solved</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50220782'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have identified the subjects in one of the earliest photographs, ending a long-standing mystery about the first people captured by photography.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员揭示了早期摄影中第一批人物图像的拍摄对象身份，结束了这个长期未解之谜。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50315987' data-tags='AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50315987' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/anthropics-ai-model-is-now-blocking-attempts-at-video-game-recreation-and-cloning-users-also-claim-non-malicious-generic-reverse-engineering-tasks-are-blocked' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s Claude AI blocks video game cloning and reverse-engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的 Claude AI 阻止游戏克隆和逆向工程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 06:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50315987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many game developers report that Claude refuses to help with projects they created themselves. The reports concern attempts to recreate or clone video games, as well as some generic reverse-engineering tasks that users say are not malicious.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>许多游戏开发者称，Claude 拒绝协助处理他们自己创建的项目。这些报告涉及重制或克隆电子游戏的尝试，以及用户认为并无恶意的一些通用逆向工程任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/uCXNTvaQDC2MRU8U37YwaV-2169-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/uCXNTvaQDC2MRU8U37YwaV-2169-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 1998 LAN party story captures how virtual gaming networks and in-person communities blend, foreshadowing the social and esports trends that followed. It highlights early hobbyist culture meeting mainstream tech interest.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1998 年的新闻报道记录了局域网聚会将虚拟世界与线下社交相结合的场景，揭示了早期玩家文化如何与主流科技趋势交汇。此类活动被视为后续电子竞技和玩家社区发展的前奏。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50289081' data-tags='Programming,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50289081' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://entropicthoughts.com/history-of-pdp-computers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brief History of PDP Computers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PDP 计算机简史</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 06:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50289081'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This history presents a timeline of PDP computers, comparing inflation-adjusted prices with memory cycle times as a proxy for speed. It describes a split in hacker culture between smaller 18-bit-and-under systems and larger 32-bit-and-over machines. Unix, C, and their libraries were shaped by the constraints of PDP-7 and PDP-11 systems, while Emacs and many Lisp variants benefited from the greater resources of the PDP-10.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份历史资料以时间线梳理 PDP 计算机，并将按通胀调整后的价格与作为速度代理指标的内存周期时间进行比较。文中指出，黑客文化曾在不超过18位的小型系统与至少32位的大型计算机之间出现分化。Unix、C 语言及其标准库受到 PDP-7 和 PDP-11 资源限制的塑造，而 Emacs 及许多 Lisp 变体则受益于 PDP-10 更充足的硬件资源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://entropicthoughts.com/image/history-of-pdp-computers.png' data-full='https://entropicthoughts.com/image/history-of-pdp-computers.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50244583' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/RFnexus/partyline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Partyline: A group voice application for Reticulum, built on LXST</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 07:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50244583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50244583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50244583'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Partyline is a group voice app built on Reticulum and LXST, showcasing a decentralized approach to real-time group audio. The project emphasizes privacy-first peer-to-peer communication without centralized servers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Partyline 是一个在 Reticulum 与 LXST 上构建的群组语音应用，展示了去中心化的实时群体音频通信。该项目强调隐私优先的点对点通信，避免中心化服务器。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1354168612/4177f96d-a5f5-4b13-8e7e-d2c0d01ccb98' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1354168612/4177f96d-a5f5-4b13-8e7e-d2c0d01ccb98' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50218199' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50218199' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aseemrb.me/blog/colors-of-a-soap-film/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Colors of a Soap Film</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肥皂膜的色彩</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 20:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50218199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explains how soap films produce vivid colors through thin-film interference, with hues shifting as thickness and viewing angle change. It riffs on how a simple everyday phenomenon reveals the beauty of optics and minimal-surface geometry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释肥皂膜通过薄膜干涉产生丰富色彩，颜色会随厚度和视角变化而改变。一个简单的日常现象揭示了光学与极薄膜几何的美妙。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aseemrb.me/og.png' data-full='https://aseemrb.me/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 09:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50233438'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UTS 39 outlines Unicode security mechanisms and considerations to prevent spoofing and other text-processing vulnerabilities, a key reference for developers dealing with internationalized text.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>UTS 39 详细描述了 Unicode 的安全机制与注意事项，帮助防止伪装和文本处理中的安全漏洞，是处理国际化文本的开发者的重要参考。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/ground-effect-vehicle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Regent&#x27;s Ground-Effect Vehicle Reimagines Coastal Travel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Regent 的地效飞行器重新定义沿海出行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 06:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50231649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50231649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50231649'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Covers Regent’s ground‑effect vehicle concept for efficient coastal travel, potentially reducing energy use and congestion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Regent 的地效飞行器在沿海出行中的应用潜力，或许降低能耗和拥堵。讨论了设计、飞行动力学以及水陆过渡的监管挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50241949' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50241949' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cp-algorithms.com/data_structures/treap.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Treap (Cartesian Tree)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Treap（笛卡尔树）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 04:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50241949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50241949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50241949'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Treap introduces a Cartesian tree where each node has a random priority in addition to BST keys. It supports efficient split and merge operations, combining tree structure with heap properties for fast, balanced performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Treap（笛卡尔树）将键作为二叉搜索树，同时给节点分配随机优先级，结合堆性质。它支持高效的分裂与合并等操作，是竞赛编程和库实现中的常见数据结构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cp-algorithms.com/favicon.ico' data-full='https://cp-algorithms.com/favicon.ico' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50210181' data-tags='Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50210181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.a16z.news/p/superhuman-hardware-design-is-the' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superhuman Hardware Design Is the Easy Part</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超人级硬件设计其实是容易的部分</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 07:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50210181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50210181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50210181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hardware that feels superhuman is only part of the challenge; the harder work lies in software integration, manufacturing, support, and ecosystem considerations. The piece argues that turning great hardware into a reliable, scalable product depends on software, supply chains, and user experience, not just aesthetics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>让硬件达到‘超人级’的体验只是难点的一部分，真正的挑战在于软件集成、制造、售后与生态体系建设。把优秀的硬件变成可靠、可扩展的产品，需要强大的软件、稳定的供应链和良好的用户体验，而不仅仅是外观设计。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50266780' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50266780' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bettascore.ai/research/reliability-reviewers-vs-owners' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reviewers rarely talk about reliability. Owners bring it up twice as often</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>评测者与所有者对可靠性的看法差异</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 15:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50266780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50266780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50266780'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study uncovers a gap between what reviewers discuss and what owners report about reliability. Owners report reliability concerns more frequently, suggesting reviews may understate real-world durability and maintenance issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究揭示评测者在可靠性话题上的关注往往少于实际所有者的反馈。所有者更频繁地报告可靠性问题，表明评测在现实耐用性和维护难点上的覆盖可能不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bettascore.ai/research/reliability-reviewers-vs-owners/image/16x9' data-full='https://www.bettascore.ai/research/reliability-reviewers-vs-owners/image/16x9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50253100' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-10-southern-africa-stenciled-art-plain.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Southern Africa&#x27;s first known stenciled rock art found hiding in plain sight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>南部非洲首例带模板涂绘的岩画被发现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 17:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50253100'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discovery of stenciled rock art in Southern Africa reveals early artistic methods and expands understanding of prehistoric communication. The find underscores the ingenuity of ancient communities and the global diversity of rock art.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在南部非洲发现的带模板涂绘的岩画揭示了早期艺术创作方法，拓展了对史前交流的理解，凸显了古代社群的聪明才智与全球岩画的多样性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50234707' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nextplatform.com/connect/2026/10/23/apc-says-let-there-be-light-with-raft-of-standards-to-speed-up-photonics-manufacturing/5304950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>APC says let there be light, with standards to speed up photonics manufacturing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>APC 提倡通过标准化加速光子制造</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 11:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50234707'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>APC advocates standardization to speed up photonics manufacturing, with a raft of standards covering components, testing, and data. The move aims to reduce ramp time for photonic devices, from integrated circuits to optical sensors, by enabling interoperability and scalable supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>APC推动通过标准化来加速光子制造，涵盖组件、测试和数据格式等标准。此举旨在提升互操作性和供应链扩张，缩短光子器件的投产周期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.nextplatform.com/5304953.jpg?imageId=5304953&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.nextplatform.com/5304953.jpg?imageId=5304953&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50291902' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警方在归还前应否清除犯罪者手机数据？——美国诉 Stathas 案</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Legal analysis discusses whether police should wipe data from a suspect&#x27;s phone before returning it, exploring Fourth Amendment implications and evidentiary rights. The case foregrounds privacy versus investigative needs in digital contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法律分析探讨警方在归还涉案者手机前是否应清除其数据，涉及第四修正案及证据权利。在数字化场景下，隐私与调查需求之间的博弈被突出。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50305218' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sketch.wakeshift.ai/games/q5ucru' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sketch Stadium – watch your agent play Pictionary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Sketch Stadium——观看你的代理玩你画我猜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 08:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50305218'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sketch Stadium lets AI agents play Pictionary, with agents taking turns drawing and guessing. Clues are worth one, two, or three points depending on difficulty. The project began as a proof of concept for sending push notifications to agents and became a standalone demonstration. Replacing line-based drawing with a restricted SVG subset made the agents perform better, including lower-cost models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sketch Stadium 让 AI 代理玩“你画我猜”，代理轮流画图和猜答案。题目按难度分别价值1分、2分或3分。项目最初是发送推送通知给 AI 代理的概念验证，后来发展成独立演示。将基于线条的绘图改为受限的 SVG 子集后，代理的表现有所改善，低成本模型也受益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sketch.wakeshift.ai/static/logo.svg?v=6a02528a1338' data-full='https://sketch.wakeshift.ai/static/logo.svg?v=6a02528a1338' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lonthq/overtone-captions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Overtone – Transcribe, correct and cut captions for any screen shape</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Overtone — 适用于任意屏幕形状的字幕转写、纠错与裁剪</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 10:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293115' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293115' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50293115'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overtone is a show-n-HN project that transcribes, corrects, and cuts captions to fit any screen shape. It advances accessibility and localization workflows by automating caption adaptation. The tool can help creators reach diverse audiences across devices with fewer manual tweaks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Overtone 是 Show HN 项目，能够转写、纠错并将字幕裁剪以适配任意屏幕形状，提升无障碍和本地化工作效率。它帮助内容创作者覆盖多设备场景，省去大量手动调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e63e0a6e0b37892340e8bb3df894b5894d38f303c0b38df1ec8268d1492aa496/lonthq/overtone-captions' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e63e0a6e0b37892340e8bb3df894b5894d38f303c0b38df1ec8268d1492aa496/lonthq/overtone-captions' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50233948' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipinfo.io/blog/how-made-mmdb-files-smaller' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we made our MMDB files a 33% smaller</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们如何让 MMDB 文件变小33%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 10:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50233948'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The MMDB size shrinkage report explains how targeted optimizations reduced file sizes by about 33%. The change lowers storage and bandwidth costs for IP geolocation services and related deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释通过针对性优化把 MMDB 文件大小减少约 33%，包括格式与数据组织方面的改进。规模缩减降低了存储和带宽成本，提升了 IP 地理定位相关服务的效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b1/57/b157bf7f-0fe2-447f-a945-12d7951755b3/content/images/2026/10/Mmdb-Technical---Social-Media-1.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b1/57/b157bf7f-0fe2-447f-a945-12d7951755b3/content/images/2026/10/Mmdb-Technical---Social-Media-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50299004' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50299004' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Millillion/lasm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Lasm – Write formally verified webapps in Lean for Node/serverless/edge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Lasm——用 Lean 为 Node/无服务器/边缘计算编写形式化验证的网页应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 19:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50299004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lasm lets developers write web applications in Lean with formal verification, targeting Node, serverless, and edge environments. It demonstrates how strong guarantees can be baked into modern web apps, reducing runtime errors and increasing security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lasm 让开发者用 Lean 编写并进行形式化验证的网络应用，面向 Node、无服务器/边缘计算环境。展示了在现代 Web 开发中嵌入定理证明保证的可行性与潜在收益。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artificial Analysis v5 introduces new benchmarking considerations that reveal the tension between optimizing for metrics and delivering real-world utility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Artificial Analysis v5 引入新的基准评估考量，揭示为指标优化与真实世界效用之间的矛盾。文章讨论基准设计可能与用户目标错位，以及为何在 AI 进展中需要更健全、多样化的评估。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://johnhendersontravel.com/txoko/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Txoko: Basques&#x27; traditional feast a gastronomic peek into a culture (2024)</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50212625'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A visit to a Txoko showcases Basque private gastronomic clubs, revealing how home-style cooking, hospitality, and social ritual shape regional culture. The piece doubles as a foodie travelogue and cultural snapshot.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Txoko 指的是巴斯克地区私家美食会所，是理解当地饮食文化的一扇窗。文章描述成员通过自家烹饪、分享餐食和社交仪式维系社区，这反映了风味传统与待客之道。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/10/26/australia/prisoner-vegemite-rejected-judge-scli-intl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prisoner loses challenge to an Australian state’s ban on inmates eating Vegemite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>囚犯就某州禁令吃 Vegemite 诉讼败诉</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 07:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50244144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50244144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50244144'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A prisoner loses a legal challenge against a state&#x27;s Vegemite ban, highlighting tensions between inmate rights and institutional policies. The case underscores how food restrictions intersect with civil liberties and prison governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>囚犯就某州禁止囚犯吃 Vegemite 的决定提起诉讼但败诉，凸显囚犯权利与监狱政策之间的张力，以及此类饮食限制在民权与治理中的法律考验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50262950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/maine-cellphone-ban-positive-changes-83bb538cf45879ce6ab4639c6cc73b7b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maine banned phones in schools to help teens focus and connect. It&#x27;s working</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>缅因州在学校禁用手机以帮助青少年专注与社交：效果显现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 10:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50262950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50262950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50262950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maine’s ban on smartphones in schools appears to improve attention and reduce social media distractions among teens, according to early data. The program underscores potential benefits and implementation challenges of device restrictions in education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步数据表明，缅因州在学校禁用手机有助于提升青少年专注度、降低社交媒体干扰。此举为教育领域的设备管理政策提供了潜在收益与实施挑战的实践观察。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://africa.businessinsider.com/local/markets/uk-to-charge-universities-dollar1200-per-international-student-including-those-from/qe5rqbe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK to charge universities $1,200 per international student</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国拟对国际学生征收每名1200美元的附加费用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 22:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50254935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50254935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50254935'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UK plans to charge universities about 1,200 USD per international student, reshaping funding models for higher education. The policy could influence international enrollment, research capacity, and the competitiveness of UK universities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国拟向高校对国际学生征收每名约1200美元的附加费用，这将影响高校经费结构和国际生的招生趋势。政策实施可能改变研究产出和英国高校的全球竞争力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Enlightenment 0.28 has been released after Enlightenment 0.27 arrived at the beginning of 2025. The project had not delivered a major feature update since then, departing from its previous roughly 12-month release cadence. The new release is described as containing many improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Enlightenment 0.28 已发布，此前 Enlightenment 0.27 于 2025 年初推出。自那以后项目一直没有重大功能更新，偏离了过去约 12 个月一次的发布节奏。新版本带来了多项改进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50276906' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cailinoconnor.substack.com/p/its-now-official-tech-is-the-new' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s now official, tech is the new tobacco</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技现在正式成为新的烟草</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 10:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276906'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses concerns that tech culture and addiction-like usage patterns resemble tobacco-era harms, fueling calls for tighter regulation and more thoughtful product design. It argues for more transparency and healthier tech design to curb societal impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论科技文化及其成瘾性使用模式与烟草时代的危害相似，推动对科技行业进行更严格的监管与更负责任的产品设计。呼吁提高透明度并打造更健康的科技体验，以减少社会影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.paradedb.com/blog/opening-a-closed-tin-part-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solving Postgres&#x27; Count Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解决 PostgreSQL 的计数问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 22:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50269668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50269668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50269668'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to speeding up COUNT queries in PostgreSQL, covering approaches like caching counters, BRIN or partial indexes, and approximate counting when exact values aren’t essential. It helps developers design scalable analytics workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>针对 PostgreSQL 的 COUNT 查询提速，给出缓存计数、BRIN/局部索引以及近似计数等方法，帮助在不牺牲可用性前提下实现可扩展的分析工作负载。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.paradedb.com/blog/opening-a-closed-tin-part-2/images/opengraph-image.png' data-full='https://www.paradedb.com/blog/opening-a-closed-tin-part-2/images/opengraph-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50248876' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Amanda_Goodall/status/2114748370954985906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft offers 60k employees a voluntary exit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软为6万名员工提供自愿离职计划</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 11:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50248876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft offers voluntary exit packages to 60k employees as part of restructuring, with potential implications for product teams and hiring plans. Analysts watch for impact on innovation and capacity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软向6万名员工提供自愿离职计划，作为重组的一部分。这一举措可能影响产品团队与招聘策略，投资者和分析师将关注对创新和产能的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50300902' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/4yk5/Ghostman' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ghostman – A fast, keyboard-driven TUI API client and webhook inspector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Ghostman——快速、键盘驱动的 TUI API 客户端与 Webhook 检查器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 00:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50300902'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal. It is local-first, requires no cloud account, uses no Electron, and is presented as a lightweight alternative to heavier modern API clients.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。它以本地优先为设计理念，无需云账户，也不使用 Electron，定位是更轻量的现代 API 客户端替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/800e73193a83ca5de876356b644dda6952adb81cbc75eeaffec87a329e6c0301/4yk5/Ghostman' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/800e73193a83ca5de876356b644dda6952adb81cbc75eeaffec87a329e6c0301/4yk5/Ghostman' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Active Directory 中寻找“僵尸账户”</p>
