<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318018' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318018' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pluralistic.net/2026/10/31/burn-berne/#unvictored' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Copyright Office is restoring copyright to something you have to work to get</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国版权局正在恢复一种需要付出创作劳动才能获得的版权</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318018'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The supplied text says the US Copyright Office holds that copyright law is intended to incentivize human creative expression. Under that position, only works involving human authorship are eligible for copyright. The author supports this stance as favorable to creative workers and free expression.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供的内容称，美国版权局认为，版权法旨在鼓励人类的创造性表达。按照这一立场，只有包含人类作者创作的作品才有资格获得版权。作者认为，这一立场有利于创作者和言论自由。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/31Oct2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/31Oct2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50212764' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qwac.com/blog/who-killed-the-ev-cert' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who killed the EV cert? A browser whodunit for Halloween</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谁杀死了 EV 证书？一个浏览器悬疑案件（万圣节）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 10:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50212764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50212764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50212764'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Halloween-themed write-up investigates the fate of EV certificates in browsers, exploring why EV status is falling out of favor and what that means for user trust and phishing resistance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇万圣节主题的博文探讨浏览器中 EV 证书的命运，剖析为何 EV 状态逐渐不再重要，以及这对用户信任与防钓鱼能力意味着什么。文章强调对站点拥有者与浏览器厂商在用户界面与策略层面的实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://qwac.com/qwac-mark.svg' data-full='https://qwac.com/qwac-mark.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50315409' data-tags='Space,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50315409' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mashable.com/science/nasa-voyager-1-communication-outage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NASA&#x27;s Voyager 1 mysteriously lost touch with Earth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NASA 的旅行者1号神秘失去与地球的联系</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50315409'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Voyager 1 missed two planned check-ins on October 18 and 19 while flying beyond the solar system. NASA’s Deep Space Network received no signal during those two days, but the spacecraft transmitted again on October 20 before repair commands could be sent. No further communication outages had occurred at the time of the report. Voyager 1 is approaching a historic distance milestone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>正在太阳系外飞行的旅行者1号于10月18日和19日错过了两次计划中的联络。NASA 深空网络连续两天没有收到信号，但飞船在维修指令发出前于10月20日恢复通信。截至报道时，此后没有再出现通信中断。旅行者1号正接近一个具有历史意义的距离里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/038UOQPV1s42HhW3C7IwTY7/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1793312007.jpg' data-full='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/038UOQPV1s42HhW3C7IwTY7/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1793312007.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50266046' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rubinobservatory.org/news/rubin-first-dwarf-planet-candidate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Dwarf Planet Candidate in Our Solar System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太阳系中新发现的矮行星候选体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 14:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50266046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50266046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50266046'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rubin Observatory reports a new dwarf planet candidate in the outer solar system, with follow-up observations to confirm its orbit and size.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rubin 观测站宣布在外太阳系发现新的矮行星候选体，正在进行后续观测以确认轨道与大小。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.noirlab.edu/media/archives/images/screen640/noirlab2626a.jpg' data-full='https://storage.noirlab.edu/media/archives/images/screen640/noirlab2626a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50274186' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50274186' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.redox-os.org/news/nlnet-rb-summary/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Redox: Ring Buffers Project Summary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Redox：环形缓冲区项目总结</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 07:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50274186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50274186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50274186'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Redox team completed a grant-funded phase to add io_uring-like ring-buffer support at the system-task level. The work, intended as a first step toward ring-buffer communication between applications and most system services, finished in September. The team credited NLnet Foundation, NGI Zero Commons Fund, and Horizon Europe for their support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Redox 团队完成了一个由资助支持的阶段性项目，在系统任务层加入类似 io_uring 的环形缓冲区支持。这项工作旨在成为应用程序与大多数系统服务之间采用环形缓冲区通信的第一步，并于 9 月完成。团队感谢 NLnet Foundation、NGI Zero Commons Fund 和 Horizon Europe 的支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.redox-os.org/img/logo.png' data-full='https://www.redox-os.org/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50279722' data-tags='DevOps,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50279722' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenewstack.io/github-7-4b-commits-september/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub saw 7.4B commits in Sep., adding 4M CPU cores+160 PB storage to keep up</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 9 月提交量达 74 亿次，扩容至新增 400 万核 CPU 与 160PB 存储以满足需求</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 12:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50279722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50279722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50279722'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub processed 7.4 billion commits in September, reflecting immense activity. To keep up, the platform added 4 million CPU cores and 160 PB of storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 9 月处理了 74 亿次提交，反映出海量的活动量。为跟上需求，平台新增了 400 万核 CPU 和 160PB 存储。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.thenewstack.io/media/2026/10/9b7c9d43-img_5916-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.thenewstack.io/media/2026/10/9b7c9d43-img_5916-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50249883' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50249883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/26/science/what-happened-to-the-neanderthals.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Happened to the Neanderthals?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尼安德特人究竟发生了什么？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 12:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50249883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50249883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50249883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research integrates genetics and archaeology to explain Neanderthal fate, including interbreeding with Homo sapiens and environmental pressures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究融合遗传学与考古学证据，解释尼安德特人命运走向，包括与智人混血、气候与环境压力等因素。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50261623' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50261623' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://harpers.org/archive/2020/01/vicious-cycles-theses-on-a-philosophy-of-news/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vicious Cycles: Theses on a philosophy of news (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新闻的恶性循环：关于新闻哲学的论断</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 09:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50261623'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay on the philosophy of news, examining how cycles, biases, and business incentives shape reporting and public perception.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章讨论新闻哲学，剖析新闻如何受循环、偏见与商业动机驱动，从而影响报道和公众认知。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318442' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318442' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://datavorous.com/writing/demoscener/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I post trained a model on Demoscenes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 Demoscene 作品训练了一个模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 11:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318442'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author became interested in how visual effects in HellMood’s MS-DOS demo “Memories” were created. They traced the effects to earlier experiments, alternative implementations, and ideas borrowed from previous demos. They then modified simple existing demos character by character to study how small changes affected their visual feel.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者对 HellMood 的 MS-DOS Demo《Memories》中的视觉效果如何产生产生了兴趣。经过追溯，作者发现这些效果源自更早的实验、替代实现以及前人 Demo 中借鉴的想法。随后，作者逐字符修改一些简单的既有 Demo，以观察细微变化如何影响其视觉观感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://datavorous.com/writing/demoscener/media/figure-01.png' data-full='https://datavorous.com/writing/demoscener/media/figure-01.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50317484' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50317484' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://a-side.social/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A/SIDE – Private photo sharing and encrypted messages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>A/SIDE——私密照片分享与加密消息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 09:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50317484'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A/SIDE provides photo, video, and everyday-update sharing through a chronological feed. It offers encrypted chats, no ads, and no AI in the app. Users can sign up with email, exchange personal invite links, and connect directly to each other’s feeds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>A/SIDE 提供照片、视频和日常动态分享，并采用按时间顺序排列的动态流。它支持加密聊天，应用内无广告，也不使用 AI。用户可以用电子邮件注册，通过个人邀请链接互相建立连接，并进入彼此的动态流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://a-side.social/og-default.png' data-full='https://a-side.social/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50237813' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50237813' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freehire.me/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Freehire, open-source search 3M+ tech jobs from company career boards</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Freehire，开源搜索公司招聘板块的300万+技术岗位</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 18:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237813'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freehire aggregates over 3 million tech roles by indexing company career pages, offering an open-source search experience. It aims to improve transparency and give developers a self-hostable alternative to centralized job boards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Freehire 将公司招聘页面的超过300万技术岗位聚合并开放源代码搜索体验，提升求职透明度，并为开发者提供自托管替代方案。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50271088' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50271088' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/10/26/billion-query-per-second-lookup-tables-in-c/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Billion-query-per-second lookup tables in C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 C++ 中实现十亿级查询的查找表</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 02:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50271088'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into crafting lookup tables capable of a billion queries per second, focusing on data layout, cache-friendly access, and memory bandwidth. Implications touch in-memory analytics, DNS, and real-time systems where precomputation trades off with runtime performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨在 C++ 中构建可处理亿级查询的查找表，强调数据布局、缓存友好性与内存带宽。此类技术对内存中分析、DNS 和实时系统等场景具有直接影响，关注的是预计算与运行时性能之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/perfect-hash-cover-1024x687.jpg' data-full='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/perfect-hash-cover-1024x687.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50251062' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/proton-to-re-interview-staff-about-ai-to-see-who-gets-to-keep-their-job/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Proton to &#x27;Re-Interview&#x27; Staff About AI to See Who Gets to Keep Their Job</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Proton 将就 AI 使用重新面试以决定保留职位</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 14:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50251062'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proton plans to re-interview staff about AI usage to determine who can keep their roles, signaling a data-driven approach to workforce decisions. The plan raises concerns about bias, transparency, and the chilling effect of AI-informed HR practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Proton 将就 AI 使用在员工中进行重新面试，以决定保留职位，体现出以数据驱动的人力决策趋势。此举也引发关于偏见、透明度和 AI 影响的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/Photo_illustration_in_India_01_Dec_2023_23335625348020.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/Photo_illustration_in_India_01_Dec_2023_23335625348020.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/models/gpt-6.1-terra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT 6.1 Terra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT 6.1 Terra</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 26, 2026 / 10:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50248075'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI expands Terra with developer-focused tooling and docs, enabling smoother model integration and governance. The release highlights ongoing efforts to balance capabilities with safety and reliability in production-grade AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 通过 Terra 版本增强了开发者工具和文档，便于模型集成与治理。此次发布反映出持续平衡能力、安全与在生产环境中的可信赖性的努力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/the-new-york-times-is-adding-more-ai-features-to-its-cooking-app-and-users-arent-thrilled/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New York Times adds more AI features to its Cooking app. Users aren&#x27;t thrilled</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约时报在其烹饪应用中新增 AI 功能，用户反应不佳</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 17:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50282766'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The New York Times is adding more AI features to its Cooking app, but readers aren’t thrilled with the changes. The move underscores the tension between enhancing personalization and preserving user control and trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约时报在烹饪应用中新增更多 AI 功能，但用户对这些改动反应不佳。此举凸显了提升个性化与保持用户控制和信任之间的矛盾。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50319061' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50319061' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.daemonology.net/blog/2026-10-31-The-Duty-of-a-Scholar.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Duty of a Scholar</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学者的责任</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 12:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50319061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author describes finding an information leak that could be turned into a practical cryptographic side channel. By mid-December, they had built a proof-of-concept exploit capable of stealing enough bits of an RSA private key to reconstruct the entire key. They then spent six months quietly notifying security teams and helping fix the issue before public disclosure. The work took priority over thesis corrections and job applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者讲述了如何发现一种信息泄漏，并将其转化为实用的密码学侧信道攻击。到12月中旬，作者已经构建出概念验证漏洞利用，能够窃取足够多的 RSA 私钥比特并重建完整密钥。随后，作者花了六个月秘密通知安全团队并协助修复问题，之后才公开披露。这项工作被置于修改论文和申请工作等个人职业事务之前。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50213990' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50213990' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dark-mountain.net/two-million-hornets/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two Million Hornets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两百万只黄蜂</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 23, 2026 / 12:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50213990'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two million hornets become a lens to examine ecological dynamics, biodiversity, and human-wildlife interactions in changing environments. The piece invites reflection on how invasive or populous species influence ecosystems and our responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两百万只黄蜂成为探讨生态动力学、生物多样性与人畜共存互动的切入点。文章通过这一现象反映在环境变化中的生态影响，以及人类如何应对入侵或大量繁殖的物种。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50270973' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50270973' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.codinghorror.com/suffering-as-a-feature/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Suffering as a Feature</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把痛苦当成设计特征</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 02:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50270973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50270973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50270973'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jeff Atwood argues that some friction and hardship in software can push users toward better outcomes, balancing usability with resilience. The piece weighs when friction is productive and when it becomes a hindrance, advocating thoughtful design trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jeff Atwood 主张软件中的某些摩擦和困难可能促使用户取得更好的结果，在可用性与韧性之间寻求平衡。文章评估何时摩擦是有益的，何时会成为负担，并主张对设计进行审慎权衡。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50268478' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Executable Archaeology: Reanimating the Heuristic Compiler (1961) and KBEmacs (1984)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可执行考古学：让 1961 年的启发式编译器和 1984 年的 KBEmacs 重生</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 27, 2026 / 19:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50268478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50268478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50268478'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores reviving vintage software to study historical programming ideas, including the heuristic compiler and KBEmacs. The work reveals how early design choices influenced modern tooling and debugging practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨通过复兴经典软件来研究历史编程思想，包括 1961 年的启发式编译器和 1984 年的 KBEmacs。揭示早期设计选择如何影响了现代工具与调试实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cairoreport.org/p/egypt-brazil-iraq-billboard-hack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers hijack Egypt highway billboard and Malaysian screens to show porn</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客劫持埃及高速公路广告牌和马来西亚屏幕播放色情内容</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318182'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hacking group told the publication that it exploited LED-display software in Egypt and abroad. The incident included the Giza billboard and screens in Malaysia, and raised questions about the billboard operator’s links to intelligence services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个黑客组织称，他们利用了埃及及其他国家的 LED 显示屏软件漏洞。事件涉及吉萨的广告牌和马来西亚的屏幕，并引发了外界对广告牌运营方与情报机构关系的疑问。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318376' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318376' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.yale.edu/2026/10/31/william-nordhaus-nobel-laureate-who-integrated-economics-and-climate-change-devoted-yale' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>William Nordhaus has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>威廉·诺德豪斯去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318376'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>William D. Nordhaus, a Sterling Professor of Economics Emeritus at Yale and the 2018 Nobel economics laureate, died on October 30 at age 85. He won the Nobel Prize for integrating climate change into long-run macroeconomic analysis. Nordhaus also spent more than 50 years at Yale, serving as a teacher and in senior administrative roles including provost. His work helped transform the economics of climate change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>耶鲁大学经济学名誉斯特林教授、2018年诺贝尔经济学奖得主威廉·D·诺德豪斯于10月30日去世，享年85岁。他因将气候变化纳入长期宏观经济分析而获得诺贝尔奖。诺德豪斯还在耶鲁工作了50多年，担任教师及教务长等高级行政职务。他的研究推动了气候变化经济学的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.yale.edu/sites/default/files/styles/opengraph_image/public/2026-10/YNews-Nordhaus-bbva.jpg?h=18f4ebcf&amp;itok=oIknhuKh' data-full='https://news.yale.edu/sites/default/files/styles/opengraph_image/public/2026-10/YNews-Nordhaus-bbva.jpg?h=18f4ebcf&amp;itok=oIknhuKh' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318063' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318063' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/ai-and-ml/2026/10/31/amazon-sends-chills-through-its-workforce-with-mixed-message-memo-about-ai-use-corey-quinn/5306804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon sends chills through its workforce with mixed message memo about AI use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊关于 AI 使用的矛盾备忘录让员工不寒而栗</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318063'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon published a memo by vice president and distinguished engineer Terk Terkowitz about the developer’s role in the age of AI. The memo was originally sent internally to software engineers and managers in September, but was published publicly later in the week described. It addresses concerns shared by other organizations about AI mandates and what happens when AI makes an engineering task worse. The memo arrives as many software engineers feel uncertain about the profession’s future.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊发布了副总裁兼杰出工程师 Terk Terkowitz 撰写的一份备忘录，讨论 AI 时代开发者的角色。这份备忘录最初于9月发送给软件工程师和管理者，之后才在相关一周内公开。内容涉及其他组织也在面对的 AI 强制使用问题，以及工程师指出 AI 让任务变得更糟时会发生什么。备忘录发布之际，许多软件工程师正对这一职业的未来感到不安。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5306808.jpg?imageId=5306808&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5306808.jpg?imageId=5306808&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276235' data-tags='Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50276235' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/news/sk-hynix-major-memory-vendor-basically-sold-out-through-2029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Major Memory Vendor: We&#x27;re Basically Sold Out Through 2029</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>主要内存厂商：我们基本卖断到 2029 年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 28, 2026 / 09:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50276235'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notes that a major memory vendor is effectively sold out through 2029, signaling tight DRAM/SSD supply and potential price pressure. The disclosure points to broader supply chain constraints and customer impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出一家主要存储厂商的供货将持续到 2029 年，显示 DRAM/SSD 供应紧张，价格可能承压。透露了更广泛的供应链约束及对客户的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/05hZqTwi694JXGJufPHslgz-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1793195492.jpg' data-full='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/05hZqTwi694JXGJufPHslgz-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1793195492.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50239122' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50239122' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maritime-executive.com/editorials/closing-the-sea-lessons-from-ancient-mediterranean-blockades' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Closing the Sea: Lessons from Ancient Mediterranean Blockades</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>封锁海域：来自古代地中海封锁的教训</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 21:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50239122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50239122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50239122'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ancient Mediterranean blockades show how chokepoints and naval power shaped trade and geopolitics, offering lessons for modern maritime security and supply chains. The piece traces tactics, resilience, and limits of coercion, translating history into contemporary context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古代地中海的封锁揭示了海上要塞与海军力量如何影响贸易与地缘政治，为现代海上安全与供应链提供启示。文章梳理了战术、韧性与强制力的局限，并将历史经验映照到当下情境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://maritime-executive.com/media/images/article/1792956042_6ade568a45e00.jpg' data-full='https://maritime-executive.com/media/images/article/1792956042_6ade568a45e00.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50316090' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50316090' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.honest-broker.com/p/is-the-music-business-collapsing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is the Music Business Collapsing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>音乐行业正在崩溃吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 06:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50316090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50316090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50316090'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The supplied discussion criticizes labels for losing touch with new talent and repeatedly mining established intellectual property. It also notes that freelance independent work is thriving, with musicians finding ways to reach audiences and earn money directly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨音乐行业是否正在崩溃，并将其困境归因于唱片公司的七个重大错误。相关讨论批评唱片公司脱离新兴人才，反复挖掘已经固化的知识产权资源。与此同时，独立音乐人的自由职业市场正在发展，音乐人可以更快接触听众并直接获得收入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!TxjT!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F560b522e-72d9-46aa-928a-df08a114a23a_1200x898.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!TxjT!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F560b522e-72d9-46aa-928a-df08a114a23a_1200x898.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oseberg_tapestry_fragments' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oseberg Tapestry Fragments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>奥塞贝格壁毯碎片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 20:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50238637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50238637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50238637'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Oseberg tapestry fragments reveal early medieval textile art, offering clues about weaving techniques, motifs, and cultural context. Analysts examine designs and textile fragments to reconstruct lost sections and understand Viking craftsmanship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奥塞贝格壁毯碎片揭示中世纪早期的纺织艺术，提供关于织法、纹样与当时文化背景的线索。学者们通过对图案与碎片的分析，尝试重建丢失的部分并理解维京工艺。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/0/0d/2029_b_2_oseberg_tekstilfunn.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/0/0d/2029_b_2_oseberg_tekstilfunn.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fromtheroad.ford.com/img/en/articles/2026/simpson-desert-ranger-super-duty-counting-dunes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Counting Sand Dunes in the Simpson Desert</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在辛普森沙漠计数沙丘</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 25, 2026 / 16:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50237014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50237014'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes using automated landscape analysis to count sand dunes in the Simpson Desert, aiding understanding of desert dynamics and resource planning. The work demonstrates scalable, data-driven techniques for remote sensing in arid regions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍利用自动化地景分析对辛普森沙漠的沙丘进行计数，帮助理解沙漠动力学与资源规划。展示了适用于干旱地区的可扩展数据驱动遥感方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fromtheroad.ford.com/content/dam/fordmediasite/img/en/articles/2026/simpson-desert-ranger-super-duty-counting-dunes/Counting%20Sand%20Dunes%20in%20the%20Simpson%20Desert%20%2811%29.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' data-full='https://www.fromtheroad.ford.com/content/dam/fordmediasite/img/en/articles/2026/simpson-desert-ranger-super-duty-counting-dunes/Counting%20Sand%20Dunes%20in%20the%20Simpson%20Desert%20%2811%29.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50316635' data-tags='AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50316635' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gamedeveloper.com/programming/anthropic-appears-to-have-ordered-ai-tool-claude-to-stop-remaking-video-games' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic orders AI tool Claude to stop remaking video games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 要求 AI 工具 Claude 停止重制电子游戏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 07:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50316635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50316635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50316635'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude users reported that Anthropic had added a rule blocking the tool from remaking games. Anthropic confirmed that it updated Opus 5.5 to steer Claude Code away from recreating someone else’s specific game. The change follows a recent wave of AI-generated remakes of classic and upcoming games. Those projects have been created both for fun and for financial gain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 用户反映，Anthropic 已加入禁止该工具重制游戏的规则。Anthropic 确认，已更新 Opus 5.5，引导 Claude Code 避免重现他人创作的特定游戏。这一变化发生在近期 AI 生成的经典游戏和即将发布游戏重制版大量出现之后。这些项目既有人出于娱乐制作，也有人以盈利为目的。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eu-images.contentstack.com/v3/assets/blt740a130ae3c5d529/blta970312a7256ca55/6ae4e702d996de0029eee354/claudeiconfeatured.jpg?disable=upscale&amp;width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://eu-images.contentstack.com/v3/assets/blt740a130ae3c5d529/blta970312a7256ca55/6ae4e702d996de0029eee354/claudeiconfeatured.jpg?disable=upscale&amp;width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-98432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CVE-2026-98432: Linux IPv4 UAF</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 02:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50314397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-98432 is identified as a Linux IPv4 use-after-free vulnerability. The issue concerns memory being accessed after it has been released.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-98432 被标记为 Linux IPv4 Use-After-Free（释放后使用）漏洞。该问题涉及访问已经释放的内存。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50293806' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50293806' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/noctua-fan-swap-calnex-rb-gps-frequency-reference/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noctua Fan Swap on a Calnex Rb/GPS Frequency Reference</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 Calnex Rb/GPS 频率基准更换 Noctua 风扇</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 11:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50293806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50293806'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author documented replacing a fan in a Calnex Rb/GPS frequency-reference unit. After removing the top cover with a Torx T10 screwdriver, they found a simple, serviceable interior containing a SpectraTime GRClok-1500, an integrated timing-grade GPS module, and a stabilized 10 MHz output.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者记录了为一台 Calnex Rb/GPS 频率基准设备更换风扇的过程。用 Torx T10 螺丝刀拆下顶盖后，作者发现其内部结构简单、便于维护，包含 SpectraTime GRClok-1500、集成式高精度授时 GPS 模块和稳定的 10 MHz 输出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/noctua-fan-swap-calnex-rb-gps-frequency-reference/calnex-rb-gps-frequency-reference-open.jpeg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/noctua-fan-swap-calnex-rb-gps-frequency-reference/calnex-rb-gps-frequency-reference-open.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我是 Proton 的员工，现在要打破沉默</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50299212' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50299212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://institute.deepmind.com/essays/the-economics-of-intelligence/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AGI Economics: Coordination vs. Competence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AGI 经济学：协同与能力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 19:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50299212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind&#x27;s essay argues that governance and coordination among agents may be as critical as raw AI capability for the trajectory of AGI. It discusses how institutions, incentives, and multi-agent dynamics shape risk, alignment, and value distribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind 的文章主张，在 AGI 的发展路径中，治理与多方协作可能与单纯的能力同样关键。讨论了制度、激励与多主体动态如何影响风险、对齐与价值分配。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/9HN-UTLrBXO4f1mW60wydtE8GjI94mPGC8NYkUGEeXMB9E8zBG4D64GEOUQomBrzwRuM1b05xhWKk0BEf090jj5SmjvmXWcnm3V6hxg55H46cVif3Q=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/9HN-UTLrBXO4f1mW60wydtE8GjI94mPGC8NYkUGEeXMB9E8zBG4D64GEOUQomBrzwRuM1b05xhWKk0BEf090jj5SmjvmXWcnm3V6hxg55H46cVif3Q=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318505' data-tags='Security,Finance,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318505' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ckpdg3ql4169o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Crypto firm Binance covers 90% of losses after $6.3B hack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加密货币公司币安已覆盖 63 亿美元黑客攻击造成损失的 90%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 11:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318505'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Binance says it has covered 90% of the $6.3 billion in assets stolen in a Thursday hack. Two analytics firms say they have strong evidence that a North Korean state-backed group was likely responsible. Binance said the remaining 10% could take longer to source in the required amounts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>币安表示，已覆盖周四黑客攻击中被盗 63 亿美元资产的 90%。两家分析公司称，有充分情报表明，幕后可能是一个朝鲜国家支持的组织。币安表示，剩余 10% 可能需要更长时间才能筹集到足够金额。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2e52/live/ccbb9490-d53e-11f1-93de-81de9e959fac.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2e52/live/ccbb9490-d53e-11f1-93de-81de9e959fac.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318169' data-tags='AI,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318169' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/ai-data-centers-could-erase-15-years-of-americas-climate-progress-by-2030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Data Centers Could Erase 15 Years of America&#x27;s Climate Progress by 2030</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>到 2030 年，AI 数据中心可能抹去美国 15 年的气候进展</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318169'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Sierra Club analysis projects that AI data centers could add 460 million metric tons of annual CO2 emissions by 2030, driven partly by off-grid gas generation. The analysis says US power-sector emissions could reach 2.1 billion metric tons in 2030, about 42% above 2025 levels. Slow clean-energy policy is identified as another factor behind the projected increase.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>塞拉俱乐部的一项分析预测，到 2030 年，AI 数据中心每年可能增加 4.6 亿吨二氧化碳排放，其中一部分来自离网燃气发电。该分析称，美国电力行业排放量到 2030 年可能达到 21 亿吨，比 2025 年高约 42%。清洁能源政策推进缓慢也被列为排放增长的原因之一。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/The-AI-Buildout-Is-Running-on-Gas-and-the-Grid-Is-Paying-for-It-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/The-AI-Buildout-Is-Running-on-Gas-and-the-Grid-Is-Paying-for-It-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50317548' data-tags='Show HN,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50317548' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jointter.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Jointter – A daily word puzzle using dots and lines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Jointter——一款用点和线玩的每日文字谜题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 09:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50317548'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jointter is a daily puzzle inspired by games such as Sudoku and Wordle. It represents Latin letters using lines connecting dots arranged in three rows, while avoiding oval shapes by using rectangles and squares. Players can refer to the displayed alphabet while solving the puzzle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jointter 是一款每日谜题游戏，灵感来自 Sudoku 和 Wordle 等作品。它用连接三排点的线来表示拉丁字母，并通过矩形和正方形而不是椭圆来处理字母中的圆形部分。玩家解谜时可以参考界面上显示的字母表。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jointter.com/board/2026-10-31.png' data-full='https://www.jointter.com/board/2026-10-31.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50302407' data-tags='Hardware,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50302407' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MENoB3Rex28' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fiber lasts decades, GPUs last three years: the AI boom isn&#x27;t the dot-com boom [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>光纤寿命数十年，GPU仅三年：AI 热潮并非点 com 弹式泡沫 [视频]</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 30, 2026 / 04:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50302407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50302407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50302407'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video argues that fiber lasts decades while GPUs cycle every few years, suggesting the AI boom is hardware-refresh-intensive rather than a dot-com style bubble. It underscores the need for sustainable hardware economics and reliable supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频指出光纤可用数十年，而 GPU 的生命周期通常只有三到几年，暗示 AI 热潮更像需要持续硬件升级的时代，而非点 com 弹式泡沫。强调对硬件经济性与供应链的可持续性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318200' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318200' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maniek86.xyz/projects/homebrew_m68k_pci.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Homebrew 68000 computer with a PCI bus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带 PCI 总线的自制 68000 计算机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 10:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318200'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The completed homebrew computer uses a 10 MHz Motorola 68000, 1 MB of SRAM, 4 KB of ROM, and a Xilinx Spartan II FPGA as a PCI bridge. It provides two interrupt-capable 32-bit 5V PCI slots but does not support bus mastering or DMA. The system is working, and its code and schematic are available.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这台完成的自制计算机采用 10 MHz Motorola 68000、1 MB SRAM、4 KB ROM，以及作为 PCI 桥接器的 Xilinx Spartan II FPGA。它提供两个支持中断的 32 位 5V PCI 插槽，但不支持总线主控或 DMA。该系统已经可以工作，代码和原理图也已提供。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://maniek86.xyz/cmsesus/uploads/image/projects/m68kpci/img_20260819_215428_c.jpg' data-full='https://maniek86.xyz/cmsesus/uploads/image/projects/m68kpci/img_20260819_215428_c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50317331' data-tags='Show HN,Hardware,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50317331' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A USB-C LTE modem that gives my computer a phone line</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：一款通过 USB-C 为电脑提供电话线路的 LTE 调制解调器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 08:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50317331'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C. With a nano-SIM and one USB-C cable, a computer can route call audio to a headset, hand calls to a script or AI agent, and send texts. The four-layer KiCad board was designed from a one-page specification and reviewed against component datasheets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。插入 nano-SIM 并连接一根 USB-C 线后，电脑可以把通话音频发送到耳机，也可以将通话交给脚本或 AI 代理处理，并发送短信。这块四层 KiCad 电路板根据一页规格说明设计，并逐项对照元件数据手册进行审核。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2a679309cf3b0f2a0c7d976f0db1de94b133988d0dd87a1372bb6e5472cb9724/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2a679309cf3b0f2a0c7d976f0db1de94b133988d0dd87a1372bb6e5472cb9724/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50317269' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50317269' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://text.npr.org/nx-s1-6021471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>After last year&#x27;s storm, this remote Alaska town is still picking up the pieces</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>去年风暴过后，这座偏远的阿拉斯加小镇仍在收拾残局</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 08:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50317269'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The remnants of Typhoon Halong devastated several remote Alaska Native villages in Western Alaska last fall, destroying infrastructure, floating homes off their foundations, and displacing more than 1,000 people. A year later, hundreds of residents remain displaced as repairs continue slowly. In the hard-hit town of Kipnuk, residents still do not know when they will be able to return.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>去年秋天，台风“海棠”的残余势力重创了阿拉斯加西部多个偏远原住民村落，摧毁基础设施、将房屋冲离地基，并导致超过1000人流离失所。一年后，随着修复工作缓慢推进，仍有数百人无法回家。在受灾严重的基普努克镇，居民依然不知道何时能够搬回去。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318882' data-tags='Web,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318882' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/devops/2026/10/30/http/3-is-faster-with-some-work-and-help-from-cloudflare/5306767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HTTP/3 is faster with some work and help from Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Cloudflare 的帮助下，HTTP/3 变得更快</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 11:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318882'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delivery Hero moved most of its user base to HTTP/3 and cut the total end-to-end response time for its mobile app home screen by 17 percent. Staff engineer Elvis Chidera described the project at the P99 2026 virtual conference. He said HTTP/3 was faster than HTTP/2 in practice, but that some slower parts required tuning. The changes targeted Delivery Hero’s mobile networking stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Delivery Hero 将大部分用户迁移到 HTTP/3，使其移动应用首页的端到端总响应时间降低了17%。该公司员工工程师 Elvis Chidera 在 P99 2026 虚拟会议上介绍了这一项目。他表示，HTTP/3 在实际使用中比 HTTP/2 更快，但其中一些较慢的部分需要调优。这些改动针对的是 Delivery Hero 的移动网络栈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5306774.jpg?imageId=5306774&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5306774.jpg?imageId=5306774&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50300049' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50300049' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/skorotkiewicz/radio-in-a-box' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Radio-in-a-box: Self-hosted internet radio with Icecast, Liquidsoap and Rust CLI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Radio-in-a-box：使用 Icecast、Liquidsoap 和 Rust CLI 的自托管网络广播箱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 22:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50300049'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio. The project emphasizes portability, reproducibility, and a minimal maintenance footprint for hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。强调可移植性、可重复性与低维护成本，面向爱好者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9925e23b9ab700275f5a00e37779d562a4f187896bcfe27cba6074388559f24e/skorotkiewicz/radio-in-a-box' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9925e23b9ab700275f5a00e37779d562a4f187896bcfe27cba6074388559f24e/skorotkiewicz/radio-in-a-box' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50318646' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50318646' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Which hobby do you think is most diverting and pleasurable?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你认为哪种爱好最令人着迷、最令人愉悦？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 11:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50318646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50318646'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The discussion asks readers which hobby they find most diverting and pleasurable. It also asks about the most unexpected benefit they have experienced from that hobby.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论邀请读者分享自己认为最令人着迷、最愉悦的爱好。问题还询问这些爱好带来的最意想不到的收获。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50297068' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50297068' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://florianwilhelm.info/2026/10/sentinel_values/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Much Ado About None: Sentinel Values in Python</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《莫须有的大惊小怪》：Python 中的哨兵值</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 29, 2026 / 15:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50297068'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Python’s None is often overloaded to mean missing, unset, an error, or a legitimate value. This creates ambiguity in cases such as caches that may store None and configuration updates where timeout=None has multiple interpretations. Sentinel values solve the problem by using a marker outside the valid value domain. PEP 661 makes sentinel values first-class citizens in Python 3.15.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，Python 的 None 经常被同时用来表示缺失、未设置、错误或一个合法值。在缓存可能存储 None，或 timeout=None 具有多种解释的配置更新中，这会造成歧义。哨兵值通过使用一个不属于有效值域的标记来解决这一问题。PEP 661 让哨兵值在 Python 3.15 中成为一等概念。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50315782' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50315782' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A language model that lives in a boot sector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：运行在引导扇区中的语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Oct 31, 2026 / 05:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50315782'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This project fits an entire inference engine into a 510-byte MBR, with the model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in following sectors. A small character-level neural network trained on machine-generated dmesg output can therefore produce Linux boot messages indefinitely. The accompanying Python trainer performs the same integer arithmetic as the assembly implementation and matches its output character by character. Debugging exposed an x86 idiv instruction overwriting the edx register used for the bias-table pointer, causing the first neuron to be wrong.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个项目将完整推理引擎装进510字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词表和字符串存放在后续扇区。一个使用机器生成的 dmesg 输出训练的小型字符级神经网络，因此可以无限生成 Linux 启动消息。配套的 Python 训练程序执行与汇编实现相同的整数运算，输出能够逐字符匹配。调试发现，x86 的 idiv 指令覆盖了原本用于偏置表指针的 edx 寄存器，导致第一个神经元出错。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5e122097ad959d06d5c9f5f6d807709793a8383bbf5658093d267b697bc53f12/kerlenton/bootgpt' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5e122097ad959d06d5c9f5f6d807709793a8383bbf5658093d267b697bc53f12/kerlenton/bootgpt' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
